1→"""Runtime setup helpers for review command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
     8→
     9→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    10→    from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap
    11→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    12→    from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun
    13→
    14→
    15→def setup_lang(
    16→    lang: LangConfig,
    17→    path: Path,
    18→    config: dict[str, Any],
    19→    *,
    20→    make_lang_run_fn: Callable[[LangConfig], LangRun],
    21→    file_zone_map_cls: type[FileZoneMap],
    22→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str],
    23→    log_fn: Callable[[str], None],
    24→) -> tuple[LangRun, list[str]]:
    25→    """Build LangRun with zone map + dep graph and return (run, files)."""
    26→    lang_run = make_lang_run_fn(lang)
    27→    files: list[str] = []
    28→
    29→    if lang_run.zone_rules and lang_run.file_finder:
    30→        files = lang_run.file_finder(path)
    31→        zone_overrides = config.get("zone_overrides") or None
    32→        lang_run.zone_map = file_zone_map_cls(
    33→            files, lang_run.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel_fn, overrides=zone_overrides
    34→        )
    35→
    36→    if lang_run.build_dep_graph and lang_run.dep_graph is None:
    37→        try:
    38→            lang_run.dep_graph = lang_run.build_dep_graph(path)
    39→        except (
    40→            OSError,
    41→            UnicodeDecodeError,
    42→            ValueError,
    43→            TypeError,
    44→            RuntimeError,
    45→        ) as exc:
    46→            log_fn(f"  dep graph unavailable for review context: {exc}")
    47→
    48→    return lang_run, files
    49→
    50→
    51→def setup_lang_concrete(lang: LangConfig, path: Path, config: dict) -> tuple[LangRun, list[str]]:
    52→    """Build LangRun with zone map + dep graph using concrete dependencies."""
    53→    from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap
    54→    from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    55→    from desloppify.languages import runtime as lang_runtime_mod
    56→    from desloppify.core.output_api import log
    57→
    58→    return setup_lang(
    59→        lang,
    60→        path,
    61→        config,
    62→        make_lang_run_fn=lang_runtime_mod.make_lang_run,
    63→        file_zone_map_cls=FileZoneMap,
    64→        rel_fn=rel,
    65→        log_fn=log,
    66→    )
    67→
    68→
    69→__all__ = ["setup_lang", "setup_lang_concrete"]
    70→

Return [] if no issues are worth flagging.\n\nSCORING METHOD (evidence-first):\n- Start each dimension at 100, then subtract only for evidenced issues in reviewed scope.\n- Suggested severity weights per issue: minor -2 to -5, moderate -6 to -12, major -13 to -25.\n- Never inflate scores to meet a target.\n\nGLOBAL ANCHORS:\n- 100: exemplary; no material issues found.\n- 90: strong; minor questionable choices exist but do not harm maintainability.\n- 80: solid but with repeated minor issues or one clear moderate issue.\n- 70: mixed quality; multiple moderate issues reduce clarity or change safety.\n- 60: significant quality drag; frequent issues or one severe issue.\n- 40: poor; systemic problems regularly impede understanding and modification.\n- 20: severely problematic; with a few generic or slightly misleading names.\n  80 = recurring generic naming or vocabulary drift that slows understanding.\n  60 = widespread ambiguity/mismatch between names and behavior.\n- logic_clarity:\n  100 = control flow is direct and necessary with no provably dead/meaningless branches.\n  90 = mostly clear with isolated simplification opportunities.\n  80 = repeated redundant branches/checks or avoidable flow complexity.\n  60 = frequent opaque or misleading control flow.\n- type_safety:\n  100 = annotations/contracts match runtime behavior across all paths.\n  90 = generally accurate with a few soft spots.\n  80 = recurring mismatch or under-specified public typing.\n  60 = frequent type contract drift and unreliable annotations.\n- contract_coherence:\n  100 = signatures, docs, names, side effects, and return behavior align.\n  90 = minor local mismatches with low downstream impact.\n  80 = repeated API contract mismatches in the module.\n  60 = contracts are often surprising or contradictory.\n- error_consistency:\n  100 = error contracts and context propagation are coherent throughout.\n  90 = mostly coherent with occasional inconsistencies.\n  80 = repeated mixed strategies across related call paths.\n  60 = error behavior is unpredictable; failures are hard to trace.\n- abstraction_fitness:\n  100 = abstractions reduce complexity across real use cases.\n  90 = generally strong with a few overbuilt layers.\n  80 = recurring low-leverage indirection stacks or wide-bag APIs.\n  60 = abstraction cost routinely outweighs value.\n- ai_generated_debt:\n  100 = little/no LLM-hallmark ceremony; comments/logging are purpose-driven.\n  90 = mostly clean with small pockets of boilerplate/restating patterns.\n  80 = repeated 1→"""Sizing and truncation helpers for holistic context payloads."""

    13→_DEF_SIGNATURE_RE = re.compile(
    14→    r"(?:^|\n)\s*(?:async\s+def|def|async\s+function|function)\s+\w+\s*\(([^)]*)\)",
    15→    re.MULTILINE,
    16→)
    17→_TS_PASSTHROUGH_RE = re.compile(
    18→    r"\bfunction\s+(\w+)\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*return\s+(\w+)\s*\(",
    19→    re.MULTILINE,
    20→)
    21→_INTERFACE_RE = re.compile(
    22→    r"\binterface\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)\b|\bclass\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*Protocol)\b"
    23→)
    24→_IMPLEMENTS_RE = re.compile(r"\bclass\s+\w+\s+implements\s+([^{:\n]+)")
    25→_INHERITS_RE = re.compile(r"\bclass\s+\w+\s*(?:\(([^)\n]+)\)\s*:|:\s*([^\n{]+))")
    26→_CHAIN_RE = re.compile(r"\b(?:\w+\.){2,}\w+\b")
    27→_CONFIG_BAG_RE = re.compile(
    28→    r"\b(?:config|configs|options|opts|params|ctx|context)\b",
    29→    re.IGNORECASE,
    30→)

    32→
    33→def _count_signature_params(params_blob: str) -> int:
    34→    """Best-effort parameter counting for function signatures."""
    35→    cleaned = params_blob.strip()
    36→    if not cleaned:
    37→        return 0
    38→    parts = [part.strip() for part in cleaned.split(",") if part.strip()]
    39→    filtered = [part for part in parts if part not in {"self", "cls", "this"}]
    40→    return len(filtered)
    41→
    42→
    43→def _extract_type_names(blob: str) -> list[str]:
    44→    """Extract candidate type names from implements/inherits blobs."""
    45→    names: list[str] = []
    46→    for raw in re.split(r"[,\s()]+", blob):
    47→        token = raw.strip()
    48→        if not token:
    49→            continue
    50→        token = token.split(".")[-1]
    51→        token = token.split("<")[0]
    52→        token = token.strip(":")
    53→        if not token or not re.match(r"^[A-Za-z_]\w*$", token):
    54→            continue
    55→        names.append(token)
    56→    return names
    57→
    58→
    59→def _score_clamped(raw: float) -> int:
    60→    """Clamp score-like values to [0, 100]."""
    61→    return int(max(0, min(100, round(raw))))
    62→
    63→
    64→def _strip_docstring(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> list[ast.stmt]:
    65→    """Strip a leading docstring from a function/method body."""
    66→    if (
    67→        body
    68→        and isinstance(body[0], ast.Expr)
    69→        and isinstance(body[0].value, ast.Constant)
    70→        and isinstance(body[0].value.value, str)
    71→    ):
    72→        return body[1:]
    73→    return body
    74→
    75→
    76→def _python_passthrough_target(stmt: ast.stmt) -> str | None:
    77→    """Return passthrough call target when stmt is `return target(...)`."""
    78→    if not isinstance(stmt, ast.Return):
    79→        return None
    80→    value = stmt.value
    81→    if not isinstance(value, ast.Call):
    82→        return None
    83→    target = value.func
    84→    if isinstance(target, ast.Name):
    85→        return target.id
    86→    return None
    87→
    88→
    89→def _find_python_passthrough_wrappers(tree: ast.Module) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    90→    """Find Python wrapper pairs via AST traversal."""
    91→    wrappers: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    92→    for node in ast.walk(tree):
    93→        if not isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef):
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        body = _strip_docstring(list(node.body))
    97→        if len(body) != 1:
    98→            continue
    99→
   100→        target_name = _python_passthrough_target(body[0])
   101→        if target_name and node.name != target_name:
   102→            wrappers.append((node.name, target_name))
   103→    return wrappers
   104→
   105→
   106→def _is_delegation_stmt(stmt: ast.stmt) -> str | None:
   107→    """Return the delegate attribute name if *stmt* is a pure delegation.
   108→
   109→    Matches patterns like:
   110→    - ``return self.x.method(...)``
   111→    - ``return self.x``  (property forwarding)
   112→    - ``self.x.method(...)``  (void delegation)
   113→    """
   114→    # Unwrap Expr nodes (void calls like ``self.x.do()``)
   115→    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr):
   116→        value = stmt.value
   117→    elif isinstance(stmt, ast.Return) and stmt.value is not None:
   118→        value = stmt.value
   119→    else:
   120→        return None
   121→
   122→    # ``self.x.method(...)`` or ``self.x(...)``
   123→    if isinstance(value, ast.Call):
   124→        value = value.func
   125→
   126→    # Walk the attribute chain to find ``self.<attr>``
   127→    node = value
   128→    depth = 0
   129→    while isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):
   130→        node = node.value
   131→        depth += 1
   132→    if depth < 1 or not isinstance(node, ast.Name) or node.id != "self":
   133→        return None
   134→
   135→    # The first attribute after self — walk back down from the outermost
   136→    # Attribute to find the one whose .value is the Name("self") node.
   137→    first = value
   138→    while isinstance(first, ast.Attribute) and isinstance(first.value, ast.Attribute):
   139→        first = first.value
   140→    if isinstance(first, ast.Attribute) and isinstance(first.value, ast.Name):
   141→        return first.attr
   142→    return None
   143→
   144→
   145→def _find_delegation_heavy_classes(tree: ast.Module) -> list[dict]:
   146→    """Find classes where most methods delegate to a single inner object."""
   147→    results: list[dict] = []
   148→    for node in ast.walk(tree):
   149→        if not isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
   150→            continue
   151→
   152→        methods: list[ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef] = [
   153→            child
   154→            for child in node.body
   155→            if isinstance(child, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef)
   156→            and child.name != "__init__"
   157→        ]
   158→        if len(methods) <= 3:
   159→            continue
   160→
   161→        # Track which methods delegate to which attribute
   162→        delegating_methods: dict[str, list[str]] = {}  # attr -> [method_names]
   163→        for method in methods:
   164→            body = _strip_docstring(list(method.body))
   165→            if len(body) != 1:
   166→                continue
   167→            attr = _is_delegation_stmt(body[0])
   168→            if attr:
   169→                delegating_methods.setdefault(attr, []).append(method.name)
   170→
   171→        if not delegating_methods:
   172→            continue
   173→
   174→        # Use the most common delegate target
   175→        top_attr = max(delegating_methods, key=lambda a: len(delegating_methods[a]))
   176→        delegate_count = len(delegating_methods[top_attr])
   177→        ratio = delegate_count / len(methods)
   178→        if ratio > 0.5:
   179→            results.append(
   180→                {
   181→                    "class_name": node.name,
   182→                    "line": node.lineno,
   183→                    "delegation_ratio": round(ratio, 2),
   184→                    "method_count": len(methods),
   185→                    "delegate_count": delegate_count,
   186→                    "delegate_target": top_attr,
   187→                    "sample_methods": delegating_methods[top_attr][:5],
   188→                }
   189→            )
   190→    return results
   191→
   192→
   193→def _find_facade_modules(tree: ast.Module, *, loc: int) -> dict | None:
   194→    """Detect modules where >70% of public names come from imports."""
   195→    import_names: set[str] = set()
   196→    defined_names: set[str] = set()
   197→
   198→    for node in ast.iter_child_nodes(tree):
   199→        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
   200→            for alias in node.names:
   201→                name = alias.asname or alias.name.split(".")[-1]
   202→                import_names.add(name)
   203→        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
   204→            for alias in node.names:
   205→                if alias.name == "*":
   206→                    continue
   207→                name = alias.asname or alias.name
   208→                import_names.add(name)
   209→        elif isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef):
   210→            defined_names.add(node.name)
   211→        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
   212→            defined_names.add(node.name)
   213→        elif isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
   214→            for target in node.targets:
   215→                if isinstance(target, ast.Name) and target.id == "__all__":
   216→                    continue
   217→                if isinstance(target, ast.Name):
   218→                    defined_names.add(target.id)
   219→
   220→    # Remove private names
   221→    public_imports = {n for n in import_names if not n.startswith("_")}
   222→    public_defs = {n for n in defined_names if not n.startswith("_")}
   223→
   224→    total_public = len(public_imports | public_defs)
   225→    if total_public < 3:
   226→        return None
   227→
   228→    # Re-exported = imported names that aren't shadowed by a local definition
   229→    re_exported = public_imports - public_defs
   230→    re_export_ratio = len(re_exported) / total_public
   231→
   232→    if re_export_ratio < 0.7 or len(public_defs) > 3:
   233→        return None
   234→
   235→    return {
   236→        "re_export_ratio": round(re_export_ratio, 2),
   237→        "defined_symbols": len(public_defs),
   238→        "re_exported_symbols": len(re_exported),
   239→        "samples": sorted(re_exported)[:5],
   240→        "loc": loc,
   241→    }
   242→
   243→
   244→def _collect_typed_dict_defs(
   245→    tree: ast.Module, accumulator: dict[str, set[str]]
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Collect TypedDict class definitions from a single file's AST."""
   248→    for node in ast.walk(tree):
   249→        if not isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
   250→            continue
   251→        is_typed_dict = any(
   252→            (isinstance(b, ast.Name) and b.id == "TypedDict")
   253→            or (isinstance(b, ast.Attribute) and b.attr == "TypedDict")
   254→            for b in node.bases
   255→        )
   256→        if not is_typed_dict:
   257→            continue
   258→        fields: set[str] = set()
   259→        for child in node.body:
   260→            if isinstance(child, ast.AnnAssign) and isinstance(child.target, ast.Name):
   261→                fields.add(child.target.id)
   262→        if fields:
   263→            accumulator[node.name] = fields
   264→
   265→
   266→_VIOLATION_METHODS = frozenset({"get", "setdefault", "pop"})
   267→
   268→
   269→def _find_typed_dict_usage_violations(
   270→    parsed_trees: dict[str, ast.Module],
   271→    typed_dicts: dict[str, set[str]],
   272→) -> list[dict]:
   273→    """Find .get()/.setdefault()/.pop() calls on TypedDict-annotated variables.
   274→
   275→    *parsed_trees* maps absolute file paths to pre-parsed ASTs (built during
   276→    the main collection loop to avoid redundant parses).
   277→
   278→    Returns a list of violation dicts with file, typed_dict_name, violation_type,
   279→    line, field (when extractable), and count.
   280→    """
   281→    if not typed_dicts:
   282→        return []
   283→
   284→    violations: list[dict] = []
   285→    for filepath, tree in parsed_trees.items():
   286→        rpath = rel(filepath)
   287→
   288→        # Collect variable names annotated with known TypedDict types
   289→        typed_vars: dict[str, str] = {}  # var_name -> TypedDict name
   290→        for node in ast.walk(tree):
   291→            if isinstance(node, ast.AnnAssign) and isinstance(node.target, ast.Name):
   292→                ann = node.annotation
   293→                ann_name = None
   294→                if isinstance(ann, ast.Name):
   295→                    ann_name = ann.id
   296→                elif isinstance(ann, ast.Attribute):
   297→                    ann_name = ann.attr
   298→                if ann_name in typed_dicts:
   299→                    typed_vars[node.target.id] = ann_name
   300→            # Also check function param annotations
   301→            if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef):
   302→                for arg in node.args.args + node.args.kwonlyargs:
   303→                    ann = arg.annotation
   304→                    if ann is None:
   305→                        continue
   306→                    ann_name = None
   307→                    if isinstance(ann, ast.Name):
   308→                        ann_name = ann.id
   309→                    elif isinstance(ann, ast.Attribute):
   310→                        ann_name = ann.attr
   311→                    if ann_name in typed_dicts:
   312→                        typed_vars[arg.arg] = ann_name
   313→
   314→        if not typed_vars:
   315→            continue
   316→
   317→        # Scan for violation calls — collect per (td_name, method, field)
   318→        hits: list[tuple[str, str, str | None, int]] = []
   319→        for node in ast.walk(tree):
   320→            if not isinstance(node, ast.Call):
   321→                continue
   322→            func = node.func
   323→            if not isinstance(func, ast.Attribute):
   324→                continue
   325→            if func.attr not in _VIOLATION_METHODS:
   326→                continue
   327→            if not isinstance(func.value, ast.Name) or func.value.id not in typed_vars:
   328→                continue
   329→            td_name = typed_vars[func.value.id]
   330→            # Extract the field name from the first argument if it's a string constant
   331→            field: str | None = None
   332→            if node.args and isinstance(node.args[0], ast.Constant):
   333→                val = node.args[0].value
   334→                if isinstance(val, str):
   335→                    field = val
   336→            hits.append((td_name, func.attr, field, node.lineno))
   337→
   338→        # Group by (td_name, method, field) for compact reporting
   339→        groups: dict[tuple[str, str, str | None], list[int]] = {}
   340→        for td_name, method, field, lineno in hits:
   341→            groups.setdefault((td_name, method, field), []).append(lineno)
   342→
   343→        for (td_name, method, field), lines in groups.items():
   344→            entry: dict = {
   345→                "file": rpath,
   346→                "typed_dict_name": td_name,
   347→                "violation_type": method,
   348→                "line": lines[0],
   349→                "count": len(lines),
   350→            }
   351→            if field is not None:
   352→                entry["field"] = field
   353→            violations.append(entry)
   354→
   355→    return violations
   356→
   357→
   358→def _abstractions_context(file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> dict:
   359→    util_files = []
   360→    wrappers_by_file: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   361→    interface_declarations: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
   362→    implementations: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
   363→    indirection_hotspots: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   364→    wide_param_bags: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   365→    delegation_classes: list[dict] = []
   366→    facade_modules: list[dict] = []
   367→    typed_dict_defs: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   368→    parsed_trees: dict[str, ast.Module] = {}
   369→
   370→    total_function_signatures = 0
   371→    total_wrappers = 0
   372→
   373→    for filepath, content in file_contents.items():
   374→        rpath = rel(filepath)
   375→        loc = len(content.splitlines())
   376→        basename = Path(rpath).stem.lower()
   377→        if basename in {"utils", "helpers", "util", "helper", "common", "misc"}:
   378→            util_files.append(
   379→                {
   380→                    "file": rpath,
   381→                    "loc": loc,
   382→                    "excerpt": file_excerpt(filepath) or "",
   383→                }
   384→            )
   385→
   386→        # ── Regex-based detectors (all languages) ────────────
   387→        signatures = _DEF_SIGNATURE_RE.findall(content)
   388→        total_function_signatures += len(signatures)
   389→
   390→        ts_wrappers = [
   391→            (wrapper, target)
   392→            for wrapper, target in _TS_PASSTHROUGH_RE.findall(content)
   393→            if wrapper != target
   394→        ]
   395→
   396→        for match in _INTERFACE_RE.finditer(content):
   397→            iface = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
   398→            if iface:
   399→                interface_declarations[iface].add(rpath)
   400→
   401→        for match in _IMPLEMENTS_RE.finditer(content):
   402→            for iface in _extract_type_names(match.group(1)):
   403→                implementations[iface].add(rpath)
   404→        for match in _INHERITS_RE.finditer(content):
   405→            blob = match.group(1) or match.group(2) or ""
   406→            for iface in _extract_type_names(blob):
   407→                implementations[iface].add(rpath)
   408→
   409→        chain_matches = _CHAIN_RE.findall(content)
   410→        max_chain_depth = max((token.count(".") for token in chain_matches), default=0)
   411→        if max_chain_depth >= 3 or len(chain_matches) >= 6:
   412→            indirection_hotspots.append(
   413→                {
   414→                    "file": rpath,
   415→                    "max_chain_depth": max_chain_depth,
   416→                    "chain_count": len(chain_matches),
   417→                }
   418→            )
   419→
   420→        wide_functions = sum(
   421→            1 for params_blob in signatures if _count_signature_params(params_blob) >= 7
   422→        )
   423→        bag_mentions = len(_CONFIG_BAG_RE.findall(content))
   424→        if wide_functions > 0 or bag_mentions >= 10:
   425→            wide_param_bags.append(
   426→                {
   427→                    "file": rpath,
   428→                    "wide_functions": wide_functions,
   429→                    "config_bag_mentions": bag_mentions,
   430→                }
   431→            )
   432→
   433→        # ── AST-based detectors (Python files only) ──────────
   434→        try:
   435→            tree = ast.parse(content)
   436→        except SyntaxError:
   437→            tree = None
   438→
   439→        if tree is not None:
   440→            parsed_trees[filepath] = tree
   441→
   442→            py_wrappers = _find_python_passthrough_wrappers(tree)
   443→
   444→            for entry in _find_delegation_heavy_classes(tree):
   445→                delegation_classes.append({"file": rpath, **entry})
   446→
   447→            facade_result = _find_facade_modules(tree, loc=loc)
   448→            if facade_result is not None:
   449→                facade_modules.append({"file": rpath, **facade_result})
   450→
   451→            _collect_typed_dict_defs(tree, typed_dict_defs)
   452→        else:
   453→            py_wrappers = []
   454→
   455→        wrapper_pairs = py_wrappers + ts_wrappers
   456→        if wrapper_pairs:
   457→            total_wrappers += len(wrapper_pairs)
   458→            wrappers_by_file.append(
   459→                {
   460→                    "file": rpath,
   461→                    "count": len(wrapper_pairs),
   462→                    "samples": [f"{w}->{t}" for w, t in wrapper_pairs[:5]],
   463→                }
   464→            )
   465→
   466→    # ── Post-loop cross-file analysis ─────────────────────────
   467→
   468→    one_impl_interfaces: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   469→    for iface, declared_in in interface_declarations.items():
   470→        implemented_in = sorted(implementations.get(iface, set()))
   471→        if len(implemented_in) != 1:
   472→            continue
   473→        one_impl_interfaces.append(
   474→            {
   475→                "interface": iface,
   476→                "declared_in": sorted(declared_in),
   477→                "implemented_in": implemented_in,
   478→            }
   479→        )
   480→
   481→    typed_dict_violations = _find_typed_dict_usage_violations(
   482→        parsed_trees, typed_dict_defs
   483→    )[:20]
   484→    total_typed_dict_violations = sum(v.get("count", 1) for v in typed_dict_violations)
   485→    typed_dict_violation_files = {v["file"] for v in typed_dict_violations}
   486→
   487→    # ── Sort ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   488→
   489→    wrappers_by_file.sort(key=lambda item: -int(item["count"]))
   490→    indirection_hotspots.sort(
   491→        key=lambda item: (-int(item["max_chain_depth"]), -int(item["chain_count"]))
   492→    )
   493→    wide_param_bags.sort(
   494→        key=lambda item: (
   495→            -int(item["wide_functions"]),
   496→            -int(item["config_bag_mentions"]),
   497→        )
   498→    )
   499→    one_impl_interfaces.sort(key=lambda item: str(item["interface"]))
   500→    delegation_classes.sort(key=lambda d: -d["delegation_ratio"])
   501→    delegation_classes = delegation_classes[:20]
   502→    facade_modules.sort(key=lambda d: -d["re_export_ratio"])
   503→    facade_modules = facade_modules[:20]
   504→
   505→    # ── Sub-axis scoring ──────────────────────────────────────
   506→
   507→    wrapper_rate = total_wrappers / max(total_function_signatures, 1)
   508→    abstraction_leverage = _score_clamped(
   509→        100 - (wrapper_rate * 120) - (len(util_files) * 1.5)
   510→    )
   511→    indirection_cost = _score_clamped(
   512→        100
   513→        - (sum(item["max_chain_depth"] for item in indirection_hotspots[:20]) * 2.5)
   514→        - (sum(item["wide_functions"] for item in wide_param_bags[:20]) * 2.0)
   515→    )
   516→    interface_honesty = _score_clamped(100 - (len(one_impl_interfaces) * 8))
   517→
   518→    top10_delegation = delegation_classes[:10]
   519→    avg_delegation_ratio = (
   520→        sum(d["delegation_ratio"] for d in top10_delegation) / len(top10_delegation)
   521→        if top10_delegation
   522→        else 0.0
   523→    )
   524→    delegation_density = _score_clamped(
   525→        100 - (avg_delegation_ratio * 80) - (len(delegation_classes) * 5)
   526→    )
   527→    avg_facade_ratio = (
   528→        sum(f["re_export_ratio"] for f in facade_modules[:10]) / len(facade_modules[:10])
   529→        if facade_modules
   530→        else 0.0
   531→    )
   532→    definition_directness = _score_clamped(
   533→        100 - (len(facade_modules) * 8) - (avg_facade_ratio * 50)
   534→    )
   535→    type_discipline = _score_clamped(
   536→        100
   537→        - (len(typed_dict_violation_files) * 6)
   538→        - (total_typed_dict_violations * 1.5)
   539→    )
   540→
   541→    util_files = sorted(util_files, key=lambda item: -item["loc"])[:20]
   542→    context: dict[str, object] = {
   543→        "util_files": util_files,
   544→        "summary": {
   545→            "wrapper_rate": round(wrapper_rate, 3),
   546→            "total_wrappers": total_wrappers,
   547→            "total_function_signatures": total_function_signatures,
   548→            "one_impl_interface_count": len(one_impl_interfaces),
   549→            "indirection_hotspot_count": len(indirection_hotspots),
   550→            "wide_param_bag_count": len(wide_param_bags),
   551→            "delegation_heavy_class_count": len(delegation_classes),
   552→            "facade_module_count": len(facade_modules),
   553→            "typed_dict_violation_count": total_typed_dict_violations,
   554→        },
   555→        "sub_axes": {
   556→            "abstraction_leverage": abstraction_leverage,
   557→            "indirection_cost": indirection_cost,
   558→            "interface_honesty": interface_honesty,
   559→            "delegation_density": delegation_density,
   560→            "definition_directness": definition_directness,
   561→            "type_discipline": type_discipline,
   562→        },
   563→    }
   564→    if wrappers_by_file:
   565→        context["pass_through_wrappers"] = wrappers_by_file[:20]
   566→    if one_impl_interfaces:
   567→        context["one_impl_interfaces"] = one_impl_interfaces[:20]
   568→    if indirection_hotspots:
   569→        context["indirection_hotspots"] = indirection_hotspots[:20]
   570→    if wide_param_bags:
   571→        context["wide_param_bags"] = wide_param_bags[:20]
   572→    if delegation_classes:
   573→        context["delegation_heavy_classes"] = delegation_classes
   574→    if facade_modules:
   575→        context["facade_modules"] = facade_modules
   576→    if typed_dict_violations:
   577→        context["typed_dict_violations"] = typed_dict_violations
   578→    return context
   579→
   580→
   581→def _codebase_stats(file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, int]:
   582→    total_loc = sum(len(content.splitlines()) for content in file_contents.values())
   583→    return {
   584→        "total_files": len(file_contents),
   585→        "total_loc": total_loc,
   586→    }
   587→
```

    60→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:

    75→def _extract_prompt_meta(entry: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    if not isinstance(entry, dict):

    87→    if is_numeric(weight):

    91→    if isinstance(enabled, bool):

    93→

    95→    if isinstance(reset_on_scan, bool):

   100→
   101→def _merge_dimension_meta(
   102→    target: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   103→    *,
   104→    dimensions: list[str],
   105→    prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   106→    override_existing: bool = False,
   107→) -> None:
   108→    """Merge one payload's prompt metadata into the shared registry."""
   109→    defaults = {
   110→        _normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   111→        for dim in dimensions
   112→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    for raw_dim, entry in prompts.items():
   116→        dim = _normalize_dimension_name(raw_dim)
   117→        if not dim:
   118→            continue
   119→
   120→        payload = target.setdefault(dim, {})
   121→        prompt_meta = _extract_prompt_meta(entry)
   122→
   123→        if "display_name" in prompt_meta and (
   124→            override_existing or "display_name" not in payload
   125→        ):
   126→            payload["display_name"] = prompt_meta["display_name"]
   127→        if "weight" in prompt_meta and (override_existing or "weight" not in payload):
   128→            payload["weight"] = prompt_meta["weight"]
   129→        if "reset_on_scan" in prompt_meta and (
   130→            override_existing or "reset_on_scan" not in payload
   131→        ):
   132→            payload["reset_on_scan"] = prompt_meta["reset_on_scan"]
   133→
   134→        if dim in defaults:
   135→            payload["enabled_by_default"] = True
   136→        if "enabled_by_default" in prompt_meta:
   137→            if override_existing:
   138→                payload["enabled_by_default"] = bool(prompt_meta["enabled_by_default"])
   139→            else:
   140→                payload["enabled_by_default"] = bool(
   141→                    payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)
   142→                    or prompt_meta["enabled_by_default"]
   143→                )
   144→
   145→
   146→def _available_languages() -> list[str]:
   147→    try:
   148→        return list(available_langs())
   149→    except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError):
   150→        return []
   151→
   152→
   153→def _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(
   154→    *,
   155→    lang_name: str | None,
   156→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   157→    """Build merged metadata for subjective dimensions."""
   158→    out: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   159→
   160→    shared_defaults, shared_prompts, _ = load_dimensions()
   161→    _merge_dimension_meta(out, dimensions=shared_defaults, prompts=shared_prompts)
   162→
   163→    langs = [lang_name] if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip() else _available_languages()
   164→    for name in langs:
   165→        try:
   166→            lang_defaults, lang_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(name)
   167→            _merge_dimension_meta(
   168→                out,
   169→                dimensions=lang_defaults,
   170→                prompts=lang_prompts,
   171→                override_existing=bool(lang_name),
   172→            )
   173→        except (ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   174→            logger.debug("Failed to load dimensions for lang %s: %s", name, exc)
   175→            continue
   176→
   177→    for dim, payload in out.items():
   178→        payload.setdefault(
   179→            "display_name",
   180→            _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim, _title_display_name(dim)),
   181→        )
   182→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   183→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   184→        if dim in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES:
   185→            payload.setdefault(
   186→                "reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS
   187→            )
   188→        else:
   189→            payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   190→
   191→    # Preserve legacy dimensions even if a payload temporarily drops one.
   192→    for dim, display in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   193→        payload = out.setdefault(dim, {})
   194→        payload.setdefault("display_name", display)
   195→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   196→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", True)
   197→        payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS)
   198→
   199→    return out
   200→
   201→
   202→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   203→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata() -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   204→    """Return merged metadata across all known dimensions/languages."""
   205→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=None)
   206→
   207→
   208→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   209→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(
   210→    lang_name: str | None,
   211→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   212→    """Return merged metadata for one language (with language overrides)."""
   213→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   214→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=normalized)
   215→
   216→
   217→def _metadata_registry(lang_name: str | None) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   218→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   219→    if normalized is None:
   220→        return load_subjective_dimension_metadata()
   221→    return load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(normalized)
   222→
   223→
   224→def get_dimension_metadata(
   225→    dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None
   226→) -> dict[str, object]:
   227→    """Return metadata for one dimension key (with sane defaults)."""
   228→    dim = _normalize_dimension_name(dimension_name)
   229→    all_meta = _metadata_registry(lang_name)
   230→    payload = dict(all_meta.get(dim, {}))
   231→
   232→    payload.setdefault("display_name", _title_display_name(dim))
   233→    payload.setdefault("weight", 1.0)
   234→    payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   235→    payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   236→    return payload
   237→
   238→
   239→def dimension_display_name(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> str:
   240→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   241→    return str(meta.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dimension_name)))
   242→
   243→
   244→def dimension_weight(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> float:
   245→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   246→    raw = meta.get("weight", 1.0)
   247→    if is_numeric(raw):
   248→        return max(0.0, float(raw))
   249→    return 1.0
   250→
   251→
   252→def default_display_names_map(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
   253→    """Display-name map for default subjective dimensions."""
   254→    out: dict[str, str] = {}
   255→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   256→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   257→            continue
   258→        out[dim] = str(payload.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dim)))
   259→    return out
   260→
   261→
   262→def resettable_default_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   263→    """Default subjective dimensions that should be reset by scan reset."""
   264→    out = []
   265→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   266→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   267→            continue
   268→        if not bool(payload.get("reset_on_scan", True)):
   269→            continue
   270→        out.append(dim)
   271→    return tuple(sorted(set(out)))
   272→
   273→
   274→__all__ = [
   275→    "default_display_names_map",
   276→    "dimension_display_name",
   277→    "dimension_weight",
   278→    "get_dimension_metadata",
   279→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata",
   280→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang",
   281→    "resettable_default_dimensions",
   282→]
   283→

   166→
   167→
   168→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
   169→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
   170→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
   171→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
   172→        effort = "low"

   174→        "codex",
   175→        "exec",
   176→        "--ephemeral",

   178→        str(repo_root),

   190→
   191→def _output_file_status_text(output_file: Path) -> str:
   192→    """Describe output file state for live log snapshots."""
   193→    if not output_file.exists():
   194→        return f"{output_file} (missing)"
   195→    try:
   196→        stat = output_file.stat()
   197→    except OSError as exc:
   198→        return f"{output_file} (exists; stat failed: {exc})"
   199→    modified_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime, tz=UTC).isoformat(
   200→        timespec="seconds"
   201→    )
   202→    return (
   203→        f"{output_file} (exists; bytes={stat.st_size}; "
   204→        f"modified={modified_at})"
   205→    )
   206→
   207→
   208→def _output_file_has_json_payload(output_file: Path) -> bool:
   209→    """Return True when the output file contains a valid JSON object."""
   210→    if not output_file.exists():
   211→        return False
   212→    try:
   213→        payload = json.loads(output_file.read_text())
   214→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   215→        return False
   216→    return isinstance(payload, dict)
   217→
   218→
   219→def _terminate_process(process: subprocess.Popen[str]) -> None:
   220→    """Terminate (then kill) a subprocess that may still be running."""
   221→    if process.poll() is not None:
   222→        return
   223→    try:
   224→        process.terminate()
   225→        process.wait(timeout=3)
   226→        return
   227→    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
   228→        pass
   229→    try:
   230→        process.kill()
   231→        process.wait(timeout=3)
   232→    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
   233→        return
   234→
   235→
   236→def _drain_stream(stream, sink: list[str], state: _RunnerState) -> None:
   237→    """Read lines from *stream* into *sink*, updating activity timestamp."""
   238→    if stream is None:
   239→        return
   240→    try:
   241→        for chunk in iter(stream.readline, ""):
   242→            if not chunk:
   243→                break
   244→            with state.lock:
   245→                sink.append(chunk)
   246→                state.last_stream_activity = time.monotonic()
   247→    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   248→        with state.lock:
   249→            sink.append(f"\n[stream read error: {exc}]\n")
   250→    finally:
   251→        try:
   252→            stream.close()
   253→        except (OSError, ValueError):
   254→            pass
   255→
   256→
   257→def _write_live_snapshot(state: _RunnerState, ctx: _AttemptContext) -> None:
   258→    """Write a point-in-time log snapshot while the runner is active."""
   259→    elapsed_seconds = int(max(0.0, time.monotonic() - ctx.started_monotonic))
   260→    with state.lock:
   261→        stdout_preview = "".join(state.stdout_chunks)
   262→        stderr_preview = "".join(state.stderr_chunks)
   263→        note = state.runner_note
   264→    note_block = f"\nRUNNER NOTE: {note}" if note else ""
   265→    ctx.safe_write_text_fn(
   266→        ctx.log_file,
   267→        "\n\n".join(
   268→            ctx.log_sections
   269→            + [
   270→                (
   271→                    f"{ctx.header}\n\n"
   272→                    "STATUS: running\n"
   273→                    f"STARTED AT: {ctx.started_at_iso}\n"
   274→                    f"ELAPSED: {elapsed_seconds}s\n"
   275→                    f"OUTPUT FILE: {_output_file_status_text(ctx.output_file)}"
   276→                    f"{note_block}\n\n"
   277→                    f"STDOUT (live):\n{stdout_preview}\n\n"
   278→                    f"STDERR (live):\n{stderr_preview}\n"
   279→                )
   280→            ]
   281→        ),
   282→    )
   283→
   284→
   285→def _start_live_writer(
   286→    state: _RunnerState, ctx: _AttemptContext, interval: float,
   287→) -> threading.Thread:
   288→    """Spawn a daemon thread that periodically writes live log snapshots."""
   289→    def _loop() -> None:
   290→        while not state.stop_event.wait(interval):
   291→            _write_live_snapshot(state, ctx)
   292→
   293→    thread = threading.Thread(target=_loop, daemon=True)
   294→    thread.start()
   295→    return thread
   296→
   297→
   298→def _check_stall(
   299→    output_file: Path,
   300→    prev_sig: tuple[int, int] | None,
   301→    prev_stable: float | None,
   302→    now: float,
   303→    last_activity: float,
   304→    threshold: int,
   305→) -> tuple[bool, tuple[int, int] | None, float | None]:
   306→    """Check for runner stall. Returns (stalled, new_sig, new_stable_since)."""
   307→    try:
   308→        stat = output_file.stat()
   309→        current_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = (int(stat.st_size), int(stat.st_mtime))
   310→    except OSError:
   311→        current_signature = None
   312→    if current_signature is None:
   313→        # If no output file ever appears, still recover from a silent hang once
   314→        # both output state and process streams have been idle long enough.
   315→        baseline = (
   316→            prev_stable if isinstance(prev_stable, int | float) else now
   317→        )
   318→        output_age = now - baseline
   319→        stream_idle = now - last_activity
   320→        if output_age >= threshold and stream_idle >= threshold:
   321→            return True, None, baseline
   322→        return False, None, baseline
   323→    if current_signature != prev_sig:
   324→        return False, current_signature, now
   325→    if prev_stable is None:
   326→        return False, prev_sig, prev_stable
   327→    output_age = now - prev_stable
   328→    stream_idle = now - last_activity
   329→    if output_age >= threshold and stream_idle >= threshold:
   330→        return True, prev_sig, prev_stable
   331→    return False, prev_sig, prev_stable
   332→
   333→
   334→def _run_via_popen(
   335→    cmd: list[str],
   336→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   337→    state: _RunnerState,
   338→    ctx: _AttemptContext,
   339→    interval: float,
   340→    stall_seconds: int,
   341→) -> _ExecutionResult:
   342→    """Execute batch via Popen with live streaming and stall recovery."""
   343→    writer_thread = _start_live_writer(state, ctx, interval)
   344→    try:
   345→        process = deps.subprocess_popen(
   346→            cmd,
   347→            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   348→            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   349→            text=True,

1→"""Holistic investigation batch builders for review preparation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review._context.models import HolisticContext
     8→
     9→_EXTENSIONLESS_FILENAMES = {
    10→    "makefile",
    11→    "dockerfile",
    12→    "readme",
    13→    "license",
    14→    "build",
    15→    "workspace",
    16→}
    17→
    18→_GOVERNANCE_REFERENCE_FILES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    19→    "README.md",
    20→    "DEVELOPMENT_PHILOSOPHY.md",
    21→    "desloppify/README.md",
    22→    "pyproject.toml",
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def _normalize_file_path(value: object) -> str | None:
    27→    """Normalize/validate candidate file paths for batch payloads."""
    28→    if not isinstance(value, str):
    29→        return None
    30→    text = value.strip().strip(",'\"")
    31→    if not text or text in {".", ".."}:
    32→        return None
    33→    if text.endswith("/"):
    34→        return None
    35→
    36→    basename = Path(text).name
    37→    if not basename:
    38→        return None
    39→    if "." not in basename and basename.lower() not in _EXTENSIONLESS_FILENAMES:
    40→        return None
    41→    return text
    42→
    43→
    44→def _collect_unique_files(
    45→    sources: list[list[dict]],
    46→    key: str = "file",
    47→    *,
    48→    max_files: int | None = None,
    49→) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Collect unique file paths from multiple source lists."""
    51→    seen: set[str] = set()
    52→    out: list[str] = []
    53→    for src in sources:
    54→        for item in src:
    55→            f = _normalize_file_path(item.get(key, ""))
    56→            if f and f not in seen:
    57→                seen.add(f)
    58→                out.append(f)
    59→                if max_files is not None and len(out) >= max_files:
    60→                    return out
    61→    return out
    62→
    63→
    64→def _existing_repo_files(
    65→    repo_root: Path | None,
    66→    candidates: tuple[str, ...],
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Return repository-relative paths for candidate files that exist."""
    69→    if repo_root is None:
    70→        return []
    71→    out: list[str] = []
    72→    for candidate in candidates:
    73→        if (repo_root / candidate).is_file():
    74→            out.append(candidate)
    75→    return out
    76→
    77→
    78→def _collect_files_from_batches(
    79→    batches: list[dict], *, max_files: int | None = None
    80→) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Collect unique file paths across batch payloads (preserving order)."""
    82→    seen: set[str] = set()
    83→    out: list[str] = []
    84→    for batch in batches:
    85→        for filepath in batch.get("files_to_read", []):
    86→            normalized = _normalize_file_path(filepath)
    87→            if not normalized:
    88→                continue
    89→            if normalized in seen:
    90→                continue
    91→            seen.add(normalized)
    92→            out.append(normalized)
    93→            if max_files is not None and len(out) >= max_files:
    94→                return out
    95→    return out
    96→
    97→
    98→def _representative_files_for_directory(
    99→    ctx: HolisticContext,
   100→    directory: str,
   101→    *,
   102→    max_files: int = 3,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Map a directory-level signal to representative file paths."""
   105→    if not isinstance(directory, str) or not directory.strip():
   106→        return []
   107→
   108→    dir_key = directory.strip()
   109→    if dir_key in {".", "./"}:
   110→        normalized_dir = "."
   111→    else:
   112→        normalized_dir = f"{dir_key.rstrip('/')}/"
   113→
   114→    profiles = ctx.structure.get("directory_profiles", {})
   115→    profile = profiles.get(normalized_dir)
   116→    if not isinstance(profile, dict):
   117→        return []
   118→
   119→    out: list[str] = []
   120→    for filename in profile.get("files", []):
   121→        if not isinstance(filename, str) or not filename:
   122→            continue
   123→        filepath = (
   124→            filename
   125→            if normalized_dir == "."
   126→            else f"{normalized_dir.rstrip('/')}/{filename}"
   127→        )
   128→        normalized = _normalize_file_path(filepath)
   129→        if not normalized or normalized in out:
   130→            continue
   131→        out.append(normalized)
   132→        if len(out) >= max_files:
   133→            break
   134→    return out
   135→
   136→
   137→def _batch_arch_coupling(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   138→    """Batch 1: Architecture & Coupling - god modules, import-time side effects."""
   139→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   140→        [
   141→            ctx.architecture.get("god_modules", []),
   142→            ctx.coupling.get("module_level_io", []),
   143→            ctx.coupling.get("boundary_violations", []),
   144→            ctx.dependencies.get("deferred_import_density", []),
   145→        ],
   146→        max_files=max_files,
   147→    )
   148→    return {
   149→        "name": "Architecture & Coupling",
   150→        "dimensions": ["cross_module_architecture", "high_level_elegance"],
   151→        "files_to_read": files,
   152→        "why": "god modules, import-time side effects, boundary violations, deferred import pressure",
   153→    }
   154→
   155→
   156→def _batch_conventions_errors(
   157→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   158→) -> dict:
   159→    """Batch 2: Conventions & Errors - sibling behavior outliers, mixed strategies."""
   160→    sibling = ctx.conventions.get("sibling_behavior", {})
   161→    outlier_files = [
   162→        {"file": o["file"]} for di in sibling.values() for o in di.get("outliers", [])
   163→    ]
   164→    error_dirs = ctx.errors.get("strategy_by_directory", {})
   165→    mixed_dir_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   166→    for directory, strategies in error_dirs.items():
   167→        if not isinstance(strategies, dict) or len(strategies) < 3:
   168→            continue
   169→        for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   170→            mixed_dir_files.append({"file": filepath})
   171→
   172→    exception_files = [
   173→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   174→        for item in ctx.errors.get("exception_hotspots", [])
   175→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   176→    ]
   177→    dupe_files = [
   178→        {"file": item.get("files", [""])[0]}
   179→        for item in ctx.conventions.get("duplicate_clusters", [])
   180→        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("files")
   181→    ]
   182→    naming_drift_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   183→    for entry in ctx.conventions.get("naming_drift", []):
   184→        if isinstance(entry, dict):
   185→            directory = entry.get("directory", "")
   186→            for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   187→                naming_drift_files.append({"file": filepath})
   188→
   189→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   190→        [outlier_files, mixed_dir_files, exception_files, dupe_files, naming_drift_files],
   191→        max_files=max_files,
   192→    )
   193→    return {
   194→        "name": "Conventions & Errors",
   195→        "dimensions": ["convention_outlier", "error_consistency", "mid_level_elegance"],
   196→        "files_to_read": files,
   197→        "why": "naming drift, behavioral outliers, mixed error strategies, exception hotspots, duplicate clusters",
   198→    }
   199→
   200→
   201→def _batch_abstractions_deps(
   202→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   203→) -> dict:
   204→    """Batch 3: Abstractions & Dependencies - abstraction hotspots, dep cycles."""
   205→    util_files = ctx.abstractions.get("util_files", [])
   206→    wrapper_files = [
   207→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   208→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("pass_through_wrappers", [])
   209→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   210→    ]
   211→    indirection_files = [
   212→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   213→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("indirection_hotspots", [])
   214→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   215→    ]
   216→    param_bag_files = [
   217→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   218→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("wide_param_bags", [])
   219→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   220→    ]
   221→    interface_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   222→    for item in ctx.abstractions.get("one_impl_interfaces", []):
   223→        if not isinstance(item, dict):
   224→            continue
   225→        for group in ("declared_in", "implemented_in"):
   226→            for filepath in item.get(group, []):
   227→                interface_files.append({"file": filepath})
   228→
   229→    delegation_files = [
   230→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   231→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("delegation_heavy_classes", [])
   232→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   233→    ]
   234→    facade_files = [
   235→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   236→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("facade_modules", [])
   237→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   238→    ]
   239→    type_violation_files = [
   240→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   241→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("typed_dict_violations", [])
   242→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   243→    ]
   244→
   245→    complexity_files = [
   246→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   247→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("complexity_hotspots", [])
   248→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   249→    ]
   250→
   251→    cycle_files: list[dict] = []
   252→    for summary in ctx.dependencies.get("cycle_summaries", []):
   253→        for token in summary.split():
   254→            if "/" in token and "." in token:
   255→                cycle_files.append({"file": token.strip(",'\"")})
   256→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   257→        [
   258→            util_files,
   259→            wrapper_files,
   260→            indirection_files,
   261→            param_bag_files,
   262→            interface_files,
   263→            delegation_files,
   264→            facade_files,
   265→            type_violation_files,
   266→            complexity_files,
   267→            cycle_files,
   268→        ],
   269→        max_files=max_files,
   270→    )
   271→    return {
   272→        "name": "Abstractions & Dependencies",
   273→        "dimensions": [
   274→            "abstraction_fitness",
   275→            "dependency_health",
   276→            "mid_level_elegance",
   277→            "low_level_elegance",
   278→        ],
   279→        "files_to_read": files,
   280→        "why": "abstraction hotspots (wrappers/interfaces/param bags/delegation-heavy classes/facade modules/TypedDict violations), dep cycles",
   281→    }
   282→
   283→
   284→def _batch_testing_api(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   285→    """Batch 4: Testing & API - critical untested paths, sync/async mix."""
   286→    critical = ctx.testing.get("critical_untested", [])
   287→    sync_async = [{"file": f} for f in ctx.api_surface.get("sync_async_mix", [])]
   288→    files = _collect_unique_files([critical, sync_async], max_files=max_files)

   297→def _batch_authorization(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   298→    """Batch 5: Authorization - auth gaps, service role usage."""
   299→    auth_ctx = ctx.authorization
   300→    auth_files: list[dict] = []

   302→        if info.get("without_auth", 0) > 0:
   303→            auth_files.append({"file": rpath})
   304→    for rpath in auth_ctx.get("service_role_usage", []):
   305→        auth_files.append({"file": rpath})

   315→def _batch_ai_debt_migrations(
   316→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   317→) -> dict:
   318→    """Batch 6: AI Debt & Migrations - deprecated markers, migration TODOs."""
   319→    ai_debt = ctx.ai_debt_signals
   320→    migration = ctx.migration_signals
   321→    debt_files: list[dict] = []
   322→    for rpath in ai_debt.get("file_signals", {}):
   323→        debt_files.append({"file": rpath})
   324→    dep_files = migration.get("deprecated_markers", {}).get("files")
   325→    if isinstance(dep_files, dict):
   326→        for entry in dep_files:
   327→            debt_files.append({"file": entry})
   328→    for entry in migration.get("migration_todos", []):
   329→        debt_files.append({"file": entry.get("file", "")})
   330→    files = _collect_unique_files([debt_files], max_files=max_files)
   331→    return {
   332→        "name": "AI Debt & Migrations",
   333→        "dimensions": [
   334→            "ai_generated_debt",
   335→            "incomplete_migration",
   336→            "low_level_elegance",
   337→        ],
   338→        "files_to_read": files,
   339→        "why": "AI-generated patterns, deprecated markers, migration TODOs",
   340→    }

   342→
   343→def _batch_package_organization(
   344→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   345→) -> dict:
   346→    """Batch 7: Package Organization - file placement, directory boundaries."""
   347→    structure = ctx.structure
   348→    struct_files: list[dict] = []
   349→    # Add flat_dir_findings directory representatives
   350→    for entry in structure.get("flat_dir_findings", []):
   351→        if isinstance(entry, dict):
   352→            directory = entry.get("directory", "")
   353→            for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   354→                struct_files.append({"file": filepath})
   355→    for rf in structure.get("root_files", []):
   356→        if rf.get("role") == "peripheral":
   357→            struct_files.append({"file": rf["file"]})
   358→    dir_profiles = structure.get("directory_profiles", {})
   359→    largest_dirs = sorted(
   360→        dir_profiles.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1].get("file_count", 0)
   361→    )[:3]
   362→    for dir_key, profile in largest_dirs:
   363→        for fname in profile.get("files", [])[:3]:
   364→            dir_path = dir_key.rstrip("/")
   365→            rpath = f"{dir_path}/{fname}" if dir_path != "." else fname
   366→            struct_files.append({"file": rpath})
   367→    coupling_matrix = structure.get("coupling_matrix", {})
   368→    seen_edges: set[str] = set()
   369→    for edge in coupling_matrix:
   370→        if " → " in edge:
   371→            a, b = edge.split(" → ", 1)
   372→            reverse = f"{b} → {a}"
   373→            if reverse in coupling_matrix and edge not in seen_edges:
   374→                seen_edges.add(edge)
   375→                seen_edges.add(reverse)
   376→                for d in (a, b):
   377→                    for fname in dir_profiles.get(d, {}).get("files", [])[:2]:
   378→                        dir_path = d.rstrip("/")
   379→                        rpath = f"{dir_path}/{fname}" if dir_path != "." else fname
   380→                        struct_files.append({"file": rpath})
   381→    files = _collect_unique_files([struct_files], max_files=max_files)
   382→    return {
   383→        "name": "Package Organization",
   384→        "dimensions": ["package_organization", "high_level_elegance"],
   385→        "files_to_read": files,
   386→        "why": "file placement, directory boundaries, architectural layering",
   387→    }
   388→
   389→
   390→def _batch_state_design(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   391→    """Batch 8: State & Design Integrity - mutable globals, signal density hotspots."""
   392→    evidence = ctx.scan_evidence
   393→    mutable_files = [
   394→        item for item in evidence.get("mutable_globals", [])
   395→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   396→    ]
   397→    complexity_files = [
   398→        item for item in evidence.get("complexity_hotspots", [])[:10]
   399→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   400→    ]
   401→    error_files = [
   402→        item for item in evidence.get("error_hotspots", [])[:10]
   403→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   404→    ]
   405→    density_files = [
   406→        {"file": item["file"]}
   407→        for item in evidence.get("signal_density", [])[:10]
   408→        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("file")
   409→    ]
   410→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   411→        [mutable_files, complexity_files, error_files, density_files],
   412→        max_files=max_files,
   413→    )
   414→    return {
   415→        "name": "State & Design Integrity",
   416→        "dimensions": ["initialization_coupling", "design_coherence"],
   417→        "files_to_read": files,
   418→        "why": "mutable global state, concentrated quality signals, initialization coupling patterns",
   419→    }
   420→
   421→
   422→def _batch_governance_contracts(
   423→    ctx: HolisticContext,
   424→    *,
   425→    repo_root: Path | None,
   426→    max_files: int | None = None,
   427→) -> dict:
   428→    """Batch 8: Governance & Contracts - docs/policy promises vs runtime posture."""
   429→    docs = _existing_repo_files(repo_root, _GOVERNANCE_REFERENCE_FILES)
   430→    if not docs:
   431→        return {
   432→            "name": "Governance & Contracts",
   433→            "dimensions": [
   434→                "cross_module_architecture",
   435→                "high_level_elegance",
   436→                "test_strategy",
   437→                "package_organization",
   438→            ],
   439→            "files_to_read": [],
   440→            "why": "architecture contracts, compatibility policy, docs-vs-runtime scope, and quality-gate coverage",
   441→        }
   442→    top_imported = [
   443→        {"file": filepath}
   444→        for filepath in list(ctx.architecture.get("top_imported", {}).keys())[:5]
   445→        if isinstance(filepath, str)
   446→    ]
   447→    anchor_files = _collect_unique_files(
   448→        [
   449→            top_imported,
   450→            ctx.architecture.get("god_modules", []),
   451→            ctx.coupling.get("module_level_io", []),
   452→        ],
   453→        max_files=5,
   454→    )
   455→    seen = set(docs)
   456→    files = list(docs)
   457→    for filepath in anchor_files:
   458→        if filepath in seen:
   459→            continue
   460→        seen.add(filepath)
   461→        files.append(filepath)
   462→    if max_files is not None:
   463→        files = files[:max_files]
   464→    return {
   465→        "name": "Governance & Contracts",
   466→        "dimensions": [
   467→            "cross_module_architecture",
   468→            "high_level_elegance",
   469→            "test_strategy",
   470→            "package_organization",
   471→        ],
   472→        "files_to_read": files,
   473→        "why": "architecture contracts, compatibility policy, docs-vs-runtime scope, and quality-gate coverage",
   474→    }
   475→
   476→
   477→def _ensure_holistic_context(holistic_ctx: HolisticContext | dict) -> HolisticContext:
   478→    if isinstance(holistic_ctx, HolisticContext):
   479→        return holistic_ctx
   480→    return HolisticContext.from_raw(holistic_ctx)
   481→
   482→
   483→def build_investigation_batches(
   484→    holistic_ctx: HolisticContext | dict,
   485→    lang: object,
   486→    *,
   487→    repo_root: Path | None = None,
   488→    max_files_per_batch: int | None = None,
   489→) -> list[dict]:
   490→    """Derive parallelizable investigation batches from holistic context."""
   491→    ctx = _ensure_holistic_context(holistic_ctx)
   492→    del lang  # Reserved for future language-specific batch shaping.
   493→    batches = [
   494→        _batch_arch_coupling(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   495→        _batch_conventions_errors(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   496→        _batch_abstractions_deps(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   497→        _batch_testing_api(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   498→        _batch_authorization(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   499→        _batch_ai_debt_migrations(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   500→        _batch_package_organization(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   501→        _batch_state_design(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   502→        _batch_governance_contracts(
   503→            ctx,
   504→            repo_root=repo_root,
   505→            max_files=max_files_per_batch,
   506→        ),
   507→    ]
   508→    return [batch for batch in batches if batch["files_to_read"]]
   509→
   510→
   511→def filter_batches_to_dimensions(
   512→    batches: list[dict],
   513→    dimensions: list[str],
   514→    *,
   515→    fallback_max_files: int | None = 80,
   516→) -> list[dict]:
   517→    """Keep only dimensions explicitly active for this holistic review run.
   518→
   519→    If selected dimensions are not represented by any batch mapping, append a
   520→    fallback batch over representative files so scoped runs still get guidance.
   521→    """
   522→    selected = [d for d in dimensions if isinstance(d, str) and d]
   523→    if not selected:
   524→        return []
   525→    selected_set = set(selected)
   526→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   527→    covered: set[str] = set()
   528→    for batch in batches:
   529→        batch_dims = [dim for dim in batch.get("dimensions", []) if dim in selected_set]
   530→        if not batch_dims:
   531→            continue
   532→        filtered.append({**batch, "dimensions": batch_dims})
   533→        covered.update(batch_dims)
   534→
   535→    missing = [dim for dim in selected if dim not in covered]
   536→    if not missing:
   537→        return filtered
   538→
   539→    # Keep fallback batches tractable; giant sweeps are expensive and often
   540→    # unnecessary when dimensions are already explicitly scoped.
   541→    max_files = fallback_max_files if isinstance(fallback_max_files, int) else None
   542→    if isinstance(max_files, int) and max_files <= 0:
   543→        max_files = None
   544→    fallback_files = _collect_files_from_batches(filtered or batches, max_files=max_files)
   545→    if not fallback_files:
   546→        return filtered
   547→
   548→    filtered.append(
   549→        {
   550→            "name": "Cross-cutting Sweep",
   551→            "dimensions": missing,
   552→            "files_to_read": fallback_files,
   553→            "why": "selected dimensions had no direct batch mapping; review representative cross-cutting files",
   554→        }
   555→    )
   556→    return filtered
   557→
   558→
   559→def batch_concerns(
   560→    concerns: list,
   561→    *,
   562→    max_files: int | None = None,
   563→) -> dict | None:
   564→    """Build investigation batch from mechanical concern signals.
   565→
   566→    *concerns* should be a list of Concern dataclass instances from
   567→    ``desloppify.engine.concerns``.
   568→    """
   569→    if not concerns:
   570→        return None
   571→    types = sorted({c.type for c in concerns if c.type})
   572→    why_parts = ["mechanical detectors identified structural patterns needing judgment"]
   573→    if types:
   574→        why_parts.append(f"concern types: {', '.join(types)}")
   575→    files: list[str] = []
   576→    seen: set[str] = set()
   577→    for concern in concerns:
   578→        candidate = _normalize_file_path(getattr(concern, "file", ""))
   579→        if not candidate or candidate in seen:
   580→            continue
   581→        seen.add(candidate)
   582→        files.append(candidate)
   583→
   584→    total_candidate_files = len(files)
   585→    if (
   586→        max_files is not None
   587→        and isinstance(max_files, int)
   588→        and max_files > 0
   589→        and total_candidate_files > max_files
   590→    ):
   591→        files = files[:max_files]
   592→        why_parts.append(
   593→            f"truncated to {max_files} files from {total_candidate_files} candidates"
   594→        )
   595→
   596→    return {
   597→        "name": "Design coherence — Mechanical Concern Signals",
   598→        "dimensions": ["design_coherence", "initialization_coupling"],
   599→        "files_to_read": files,
   600→        "why": "; ".join(why_parts),
   601→        "total_candidate_files": total_candidate_files,
   602→    }
   603→
   604→
   605→__all__ = ["batch_concerns", "build_investigation_batches", "filter_batches_to_dimensions"]
   606→

    94→    for result in batch_results:
    95→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
    96→            payload = result.to_dict()
    97→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
    98→                normalized_results.append(payload)
    99→                continue
   100→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   101→            normalized_results.append(result)
   102→    return batch_core_mod.merge_batch_results(
   103→        normalized_results,
   104→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   105→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
   106→    )

    32→def _append_run_log(run_log_path: Path, message: str) -> None:
    33→    """Append a timestamped message to the run log file."""
    34→    line = f"{datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec='seconds')} {message}\n"
    35→    try:
    36→        with run_log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    37→            handle.write(line)
    38→    except OSError as exc:
    39→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"append review run log {run_log_path}", exc)
    40→        if run_log_path not in _RUN_LOG_WARNED_PATHS:
    41→            _RUN_LOG_WARNED_PATHS.add(run_log_path)
    42→            warn_best_effort(
    43→                f"Could not append review run log at {run_log_path}: {exc}"
    44→            )
    45→        return
    46→
    47→
    48→@dataclass
    49→class _ProgressCtx:
    50→    """Shared mutable state for the batch progress reporter."""
    51→
    52→    run_log_path: Path
    53→    colorize_fn: Callable[[str, str], str]
    54→    total_batches: int
    55→    batch_positions: dict[int, int]
    56→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]]
    57→    stall_warning_seconds: int
    58→    stall_warned_batches: set[int]
    59→
    60→
    61→def _report_progress(
    62→    ctx: _ProgressCtx,
    63→    batch_index: int | object,
    64→    event: str | None = None,
    65→    code: int | None = None,
    66→    **details,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    """Handle batch progress events (heartbeat, queued, start, done)."""
    69→    if event is None and hasattr(batch_index, "event"):
    70→        progress_event = batch_index
    71→        event = str(getattr(progress_event, "event", "")).strip()
    72→        code = getattr(progress_event, "code", code)
    73→        details_obj = getattr(progress_event, "details", {})
    74→        details = details_obj if isinstance(details_obj, dict) else {}
    75→        batch_index = int(getattr(progress_event, "batch_index", -1))
    76→    else:
    77→        batch_index = int(batch_index) if isinstance(batch_index, int | float) else -1
    78→
    79→    if not event:
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    if event == "heartbeat":
    83→        _handle_heartbeat_event(ctx, details)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    position = ctx.batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
    87→    key = str(batch_index + 1)
    88→    state = ctx.batch_status.setdefault(
    89→        key, {"position": position, "status": "pending"},
    90→    )
    91→    if event == "queued":
    92→        state["status"] = "queued"
    93→        print(ctx.colorize_fn(f"  Batch {position}/{ctx.total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})", "dim"))
    94→        _append_run_log(ctx.run_log_path, f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{ctx.total_batches}")
    95→        return
    96→    if event == "start":
    97→        state["status"] = "running"
    98→        state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    99→        print(ctx.colorize_fn(f"  Batch {position}/{ctx.total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})", "dim"))
   100→        _append_run_log(ctx.run_log_path, f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{ctx.total_batches}")
   101→        return
   102→    if event == "done":
   103→        _handle_done_event(ctx, batch_index, position, state, code, details)

   106→def _handle_heartbeat_event(ctx: _ProgressCtx, details: dict) -> None:
   107→    """Process a heartbeat progress event."""
   108→    active = details.get("active_batches")
   109→    queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])

   122→            secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   123→        segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   124→    if len(active) > 6:

   132→        )

   139→    )

   148→            ctx.stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)

    82→def _rel_list(s) -> list[str]:
    83→    """Normalize a set or list of paths to sorted relative paths (max 10)."""
    84→    if isinstance(s, set):
    85→        return sorted(rel(x) for x in s)[:10]
    86→    return [rel(x) for x in list(s)[:10]]
    87→
    88→
    89→def _normalize_max_files(value: Any) -> int | None:
    90→    """Normalize max_files input: None/<=0 means unlimited."""
    91→    if value in (None, ""):
    92→        return None
    93→    parsed = int(value)
    94→    return parsed if parsed > 0 else None
    95→
    96→
    97→def prepare_review(
    98→    path: Path,
    99→    lang: object,
   100→    state: dict,
   101→    options: ReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   102→) -> dict[str, object]:
   103→    """Prepare review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict.
   104→
   105→    If *files* is provided, skip file_finder (avoids redundant filesystem walks
   106→    when the caller already has the file list, e.g. from _setup_lang).
   107→    """
   108→    resolved_options = options or ReviewPrepareOptions()
   109→    resolved_options.max_files = _normalize_max_files(resolved_options.max_files)
   110→    all_files = (
   111→        resolved_options.files
   112→        if resolved_options.files is not None
   113→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
   114→    )
   115→
   116→    # Enable file cache for entire prepare operation — context building,
   117→    # file selection, and content extraction all read the same files.
   118→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
   119→    if not already_cached:
   120→        enable_file_cache()
   121→    try:
   122→        context = build_review_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   123→        selected = select_files_for_review(
   124→            lang,
   125→            path,
   126→            state,
   127→            options=ReviewSelectionOptions(
   128→                max_files=resolved_options.max_files,
   129→                max_age_days=resolved_options.max_age_days,
   130→                force_refresh=resolved_options.force_refresh,
   131→                files=all_files,
   132→            ),
   133→        )
   134→        file_requests = _build_file_requests(selected, lang, state)
   135→    finally:
   136→        if not already_cached:
   137→            disable_file_cache()
   138→
   139→    default_dims, dimension_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   140→    dims = resolve_dimensions(
   141→        cli_dimensions=resolved_options.dimensions,
   142→        config_dimensions=resolved_options.config_dimensions,
   143→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   144→    )
   145→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance(lang.name)
   146→    valid_dims = set(dimension_prompts)
   147→    invalid_requested = [
   148→        dim for dim in (resolved_options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   149→    ]
   150→    invalid_config = [
   151→        dim
   152→        for dim in (resolved_options.config_dimensions or [])
   153→        if dim not in valid_dims
   154→    ]
   155→
   156→    return {
   157→        "command": "review",
   158→        "language": lang.name,
   159→        "dimensions": dims,
   160→        "dimension_prompts": {
   161→            d: dimension_prompts[d] for d in dims if d in dimension_prompts
   162→        },
   163→        "lang_guidance": lang_guide,
   164→        "context": serialize_context(context),
   165→        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   166→        "files": file_requests,
   167→        "total_candidates": len(file_requests),
   168→        "cache_status": {
   169→            "fresh": count_fresh(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   170→            "stale": count_stale(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   171→            "new": len(file_requests),
   172→        },
   173→        "invalid_dimensions": {
   174→            "requested": invalid_requested,
   175→            "config": invalid_config,
   176→        },
   177→    }
   178→
   179→
   180→def _build_file_requests(files: list[str], lang, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   181→    """Build per-file review request dicts."""
   182→    file_requests = []
   183→    for filepath in files:
   184→        content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
   185→        if content is None:
   186→            continue
   187→
   188→        rpath = rel(filepath)
   189→        zone = "production"
