```
   345→    dict[str, float],
   346→]:
   347→    """Validate and normalize one batch payload."""
   348→    if "assessments" not in payload:
   349→        raise ValueError("payload missing required key: assessments")
   350→    if "findings" not in payload:
   351→        raise ValueError("payload missing required key: findings")
   352→
   353→    raw_assessments = payload.get("assessments")
   354→    if not isinstance(raw_assessments, dict):
   355→        raise ValueError("assessments must be an object")
   356→
   357→    raw_dimension_notes = payload.get("dimension_notes", {})
   358→    if not isinstance(raw_dimension_notes, dict):
   359→        raise ValueError("dimension_notes must be an object")
   360→
   361→    assessments: dict[str, float] = {}
   362→    dimension_notes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   363→    high_score_missing_issue_note = 0.0
   364→    for key, value in raw_assessments.items():
   365→        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key:
   366→            continue
   367→        if key not in allowed_dims:
   368→            continue
   369→        if isinstance(value, bool):
   370→            continue
   371→        if not isinstance(value, int | float):
   372→            continue
   373→        score = round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
   374→
   375→        note_raw = raw_dimension_notes.get(key)
   376→        evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note = (
   377→            _validate_dimension_note(key, note_raw)
   378→        )
   379→        assert isinstance(note_raw, dict)
   380→        if score > HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD and not issues_note:
   381→            high_score_missing_issue_note += 1
   382→
   383→        normalized_sub_axes: dict[str, float] = {}
   384→        if key == "abstraction_fitness" and isinstance(note_raw, dict):
   385→            normalized_sub_axes = _normalize_abstraction_sub_axes(
   386→                note_raw, abstraction_sub_axes
   387→            )
   388→
   389→        assessments[key] = score
   390→        dimension_notes[key] = {
   391→            "evidence": [str(item).strip() for item in evidence if str(item).strip()],
   392→            "impact_scope": impact_scope.strip(),
   393→            "fix_scope": fix_scope.strip(),
   394→            "confidence": confidence,
   395→            DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY: issues_note,
   396→        }
   397→        if normalized_sub_axes:
   398→            dimension_notes[key]["sub_axes"] = normalized_sub_axes
   399→
   400→    findings = _normalize_findings(
   401→        payload.get("findings"),
   402→        dimension_notes,
   403→        max_batch_findings=max_batch_findings,
   404→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   405→        low_score_dimensions=_low_score_dimensions(assessments),
   406→    )
   407→    _enforce_low_score_findings(assessments=assessments, findings=findings)
   408→
   409→    quality = _compute_batch_quality(
   410→        assessments,
   411→        findings,
   412→        dimension_notes,
   413→        allowed_dims,
   414→        high_score_missing_issue_note,
   415→    )
   416→    return (
   417→        assessments,
   418→        [finding.to_payload() for finding in findings],
   419→        dimension_notes,
   420→        quality,
   421→    )
   422→
   423→
   424→def assessment_weight(
   425→    *,
   426→    dimension: str,
   427→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   428→    dimension_notes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   429→) -> float:
   430→    """Evidence-weighted assessment score weight with a neutral floor.
   431→
   432→    Weighting is evidence-based and score-independent: the raw score does not
   433→    influence how much weight a batch contributes during merge.
   434→    """
   435→    note = dimension_notes.get(dimension, {})
   436→    note_evidence = len(note.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(note, dict) else 0
   437→    finding_count = sum(
   438→        1
   439→        for finding in findings
   440→        if str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip() == dimension
   441→    )
   442→    return float(1 + note_evidence + finding_count)
   443→
   444→
   445→def _finding_pressure_by_dimension(
   446→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   447→    *,
   448→    dimension_notes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   449→) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, int]]:
   450→    """Summarize how strongly findings should pull dimension scores down."""
   451→    return _DIMENSION_SCORER.finding_pressure_by_dimension(
   452→        findings,
   453→        dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   454→    )
   455→
   456→
   457→def _accumulate_batch_scores(
   458→    result: dict[str, Any],
   459→    *,
   460→    score_buckets: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   461→    score_raw_by_dim: dict[str, list[float]],
   462→    merged_dimension_notes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   463→    abstraction_axis_scores: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   464→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   465→) -> None:
   466→    """Accumulate assessment scores, dimension notes, and sub-axis data from one batch."""
   467→    result_findings = result.get("findings", [])
   468→    result_notes = result.get("dimension_notes", {})
   469→    for key, score in result.get("assessments", {}).items():
   470→        if isinstance(score, bool):
   471→            continue
   472→        score_value = float(score)
   473→        weight = assessment_weight(
   474→            dimension=key,
   475→            findings=result_findings,
   476→            dimension_notes=result_notes,
   477→        )
   478→        score_buckets.setdefault(key, []).append((score_value, weight))
   479→        score_raw_by_dim.setdefault(key, []).append(score_value)
   480→
   481→        note = result_notes.get(key)
   482→        existing = merged_dimension_notes.get(key)
   483→        existing_evidence = (
   484→            len(existing.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(existing, dict) else -1
   485→        )
   486→        current_evidence = (
   487→            len(note.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(note, dict) else -1
   488→        )
   489→        if current_evidence > existing_evidence:
   490→            merged_dimension_notes[key] = note
   491→
   492→        if key == "abstraction_fitness" and isinstance(note, dict):
   493→            sub_axes = note.get("sub_axes")
   494→            if isinstance(sub_axes, dict):
   495→                for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
   496→                    axis_score = sub_axes.get(axis)
   497→                    if isinstance(axis_score, bool) or not isinstance(
   498→                        axis_score, int | float
   499→                    ):
   500→                        continue
   501→                    abstraction_axis_scores[axis].append(
   502→                        (float(axis_score), weight)
   503→                    )
   504→
   505→
   506→def _normalize_word_set(text: str) -> set[str]:
   507→    """Tokenize text into a normalized word set for light concept matching."""
   508→    words = "".join(ch.lower() if ch.isalnum() else " " for ch in text).split()
   509→    return {word for word in words if len(word) >= 3}
   510→
   511→
   512→def _finding_identity_key(finding: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   513→    """Build a stable concept key; prefer dimension+identifier when available."""
   514→    dim = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   515→    ident = str(finding.get("identifier", "")).strip()
   516→    if ident:
   517→        return f"{dim}::{ident}"
   518→    summary = str(finding.get("summary", "")).strip()
   519→    summary_terms = sorted(_normalize_word_set(summary))
   520→    if summary_terms:
   521→        return f"{dim}::summary::{','.join(summary_terms[:8])}"
   522→    return f"{dim}::{summary}"
   523→
   524→
   525→def _merge_finding_payload(existing: dict[str, Any], incoming: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   526→    """Merge two concept-equivalent findings into the existing payload."""
   527→    for field in ("related_files", "evidence"):
   528→        merged_values: list[str] = []
   529→        seen: set[str] = set()
   530→        for source in (existing.get(field), incoming.get(field)):
   531→            if not isinstance(source, list):
   532→                continue
   533→            for item in source:
   534→                text = str(item).strip()
   535→                if not text or text in seen:
   536→                    continue
   537→                seen.add(text)
   538→                merged_values.append(text)
   539→        if merged_values:
   540→            existing[field] = merged_values
   541→
   542→    # Prefer richer summary/suggestion text when they differ.
   543→    existing_summary = str(existing.get("summary", "")).strip()
   544→    incoming_summary = str(incoming.get("summary", "")).strip()
   545→    if len(incoming_summary) > len(existing_summary):
   546→        existing["summary"] = incoming_summary
   547→
   548→    existing_suggestion = str(existing.get("suggestion", "")).strip()
   549→    incoming_suggestion = str(incoming.get("suggestion", "")).strip()
   550→    if len(incoming_suggestion) > len(existing_suggestion):
   551→        existing["suggestion"] = incoming_suggestion
   552→
   553→    merged_from = existing.get("merged_from")
   554→    if not isinstance(merged_from, list):
   555→        merged_from = []
   556→    incoming_identifier = str(incoming.get("identifier", "")).strip()
   557→    if incoming_identifier and incoming_identifier not in merged_from:
   558→        merged_from.append(incoming_identifier)
   559→    if merged_from:
   560→        existing["merged_from"] = merged_from
   561→
   562→
   563→def _accumulate_batch_findings(
   564→    result: dict[str, Any],
   565→    finding_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   566→) -> None:
   567→    """Deduplicate and accumulate findings from one batch into finding_map."""
   568→    for finding in result.get("findings", []):
   569→        [REDACTED](finding)
   570→        existing = finding_map.get(dedupe_key)
   571→        if existing is None:
   572→            finding_map[dedupe_key] = finding
   573→            continue
   574→        _merge_finding_payload(existing, finding)
   575→
   576→
   577→def _accumulate_batch_quality(
   578→    result: dict[str, Any],
   579→    *,
   580→    coverage_values: list[float],
   581→    evidence_density_values: list[float],
   582→) -> float:
   583→    """Accumulate quality metrics from one batch. Returns high-score-missing-issues delta."""
   584→    quality = result.get("quality", {})
   585→    if not isinstance(quality, dict):
   586→        return 0.0
   587→    coverage = quality.get("dimension_coverage")
   588→    density = quality.get("evidence_density")
   589→    missing_issue_note = quality.get(REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY)
   590→    if not isinstance(missing_issue_note, int | float):
   591→        missing_issue_note = quality.get(
   592→            LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
   593→        )
   594→    if isinstance(coverage, int | float):
   595→        coverage_values.append(float(coverage))
   596→    if isinstance(density, int | float):
   597→        evidence_density_values.append(float(density))
   598→    return (
   599→        float(missing_issue_note)
   600→        if isinstance(missing_issue_note, int | float)
   601→        else 0.0
   602→    )
   603→
   604→
   605→def _compute_merged_assessments(
   606→    score_buckets: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   607→    score_raw_by_dim: dict[str, list[float]],
   608→    finding_pressure_by_dim: dict[str, float],
   609→    finding_count_by_dim: dict[str, int],
   610→) -> dict[str, float]:
   611→    """Compute pressure-adjusted weighted mean for each dimension."""
   612→    return _DIMENSION_SCORER.merge_scores(
   613→        score_buckets,
   614→        score_raw_by_dim,
   615→        finding_pressure_by_dim,
   616→        finding_count_by_dim,
   617→    )
   618→
   619→
   620→def _compute_abstraction_components(
   621→    merged_assessments: dict[str, float],
   622→    abstraction_axis_scores: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   623→    *,
   624→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   625→    abstraction_component_names: dict[str, str],
   626→) -> dict[str, float] | None:
   627→    """Compute weighted abstraction sub-axis component scores.
   628→
   629→    Returns component_scores dict, or None if abstraction_fitness is not assessed.
   630→    """
   631→    abstraction_score = merged_assessments.get("abstraction_fitness")
   632→    if abstraction_score is None:
   633→        return None
   634→
   635→    component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   636→    for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
   637→        weighted = abstraction_axis_scores.get(axis, [])
   638→        if not weighted:
   639→            continue
   640→        numerator = sum(score * weight for score, weight in weighted)
   641→        denominator = sum(weight for _, weight in weighted)
   642→        if denominator <= 0:
   643→            continue
   644→        component_scores[abstraction_component_names[axis]] = round(
   645→            max(0.0, min(100.0, numerator / denominator)),
   646→            1,
   647→        )
   648→    return component_scores if component_scores else None
   649→
   650→
   651→def merge_batch_results(
   652→    batch_results: list[dict[str, Any]],
   653→    *,
   654→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   655→    abstraction_component_names: dict[str, str],
   656→) -> dict[str, object]:
   657→    """Deterministically merge assessments/findings across batch outputs."""
   658→    score_buckets: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]] = {}
   659→    score_raw_by_dim: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
   660→    finding_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   661→    merged_dimension_notes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   662→    coverage_values: list[float] = []
   663→    evidence_density_values: list[float] = []
   664→    high_score_missing_issue_note_total = 0.0
   665→    abstraction_axis_scores: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]] = {
   666→        axis: [] for axis in abstraction_sub_axes
   667→    }
   668→
   669→    for result in batch_results:
   670→        _accumulate_batch_scores(
   671→            result,
   672→            score_buckets=score_buckets,
   673→            score_raw_by_dim=score_raw_by_dim,
   674→            merged_dimension_notes=merged_dimension_notes,
   675→            abstraction_axis_scores=abstraction_axis_scores,
   676→            abstraction_sub_axes=abstraction_sub_axes,
   677→        )
   678→        _accumulate_batch_findings(result, finding_map)
   679→        high_score_missing_issue_note_total += _accumulate_batch_quality(
   680→            result,
   681→            coverage_values=coverage_values,
   682→            evidence_density_values=evidence_density_values,
   683→        )
   684→
   685→    merged_findings = list(finding_map.values())
   686→    finding_pressure_by_dim, finding_count_by_dim = _finding_pressure_by_dimension(
   687→        merged_findings,
   688→        dimension_notes=merged_dimension_notes,
   689→    )
   690→
   691→    merged_assessments = _compute_merged_assessments(
   692→        score_buckets, score_raw_by_dim, finding_pressure_by_dim, finding_count_by_dim
   693→    )
   694→
   695→    merged_assessment_payload: dict[str, float | dict[str, object]] = {
   696→        key: value for key, value in merged_assessments.items()
   697→    }
   698→    component_scores = _compute_abstraction_components(
   699→        merged_assessments,
   700→        abstraction_axis_scores,
   701→        abstraction_sub_axes=abstraction_sub_axes,
   702→        abstraction_component_names=abstraction_component_names,
   703→    )
   704→    if component_scores is not None:
   705→        merged_assessment_payload["abstraction_fitness"] = {
   706→            "score": merged_assessments["abstraction_fitness"],
   707→            "components": list(component_scores),
   708→            "component_scores": component_scores,
   709→        }
   710→
   711→    return {
   712→        "assessments": merged_assessment_payload,
   713→        "dimension_notes": merged_dimension_notes,
   714→        "findings": merged_findings,
   715→        "review_quality": {
   716→            "batch_count": len(batch_results),
   717→            "dimension_coverage": round(
   718→                sum(coverage_values) / max(len(coverage_values), 1),
   719→                3,
   720→            ),
   721→            "evidence_density": round(
   722→                sum(evidence_density_values) / max(len(evidence_density_values), 1),
   723→                3,
   724→            ),
   725→            REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY: int(
   726→                high_score_missing_issue_note_total
   727→            ),
   728→            "finding_pressure": round(sum(finding_pressure_by_dim.values()), 3),
   729→            "dimensions_with_findings": len(finding_count_by_dim),
   730→        },
   731→    }
   732→
   733→
   734→def build_batch_prompt(
   735→    *,
   736→    repo_root: Path,
   737→    packet_path: Path,
   738→    batch_index: int,
   739→    batch: dict[str, object],
   740→) -> str:
   741→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   742→    return render_batch_prompt(
   743→        repo_root=repo_root,
   744→        packet_path=packet_path,
   745→        batch_index=batch_index,
   746→        batch=batch,
   747→    )
   748→
   749→
   750→__all__ = [
   751→    "assessment_weight",
   752→    "build_batch_prompt",
   753→    "extract_json_payload",
   754→    "merge_batch_results",
   755→    "normalize_batch_result",
   756→    "parse_batch_selection",
   757→]
   758→
```

```
   197→def _do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   198→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   199→    runtime_project_root = _runtime_project_root()
   200→    batch_timeout_seconds = coerce_positive_int(
   201→        getattr(args, "batch_timeout_seconds", None),
   202→        default=CODEX_BATCH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,

   204→    )

   216→        default=15.0,
   217→    )

   220→        if batch_heartbeat_seconds > 0
   221→        else 5.0
   222→    )
   223→    batch_stall_kill_seconds = coerce_non_negative_int(
   224→        getattr(args, "batch_stall_kill_seconds", None),
   225→        default=CODEX_BATCH_STALL_KILL_SECONDS,
   226→    )
   227→

   229→        return runner_helpers_mod.prepare_run_artifacts(

   232→            batches=batches,
```

```
691→def build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
   692→    """Public wrapper for blind packet sanitization."""
   693→    return _build_blind_packet(packet)
   694→
   695→
   696→def _sanitize_blind_config(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   697→    """Drop score/target hints from config while preserving unrelated options."""
   698→    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
   699→    for key, value in config.items():
   700→        lowered = key.strip().lower()
   701→        if not lowered:
   702→            continue
   703→        if lowered in _BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS:
   704→            continue
   705→        if "target" in lowered:
   706→            continue
   707→        if lowered.endswith("_score"):
   708→            continue
   709→        sanitized[key] = value
   710→    return sanitized
   711→
   712→
   713→def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
   714→    """Compute sha256 hex digest for path contents (or None on read failure)."""
   715→    try:
   716→        data = path.read_bytes()
   717→    except OSError:
   718→        return None
   719→    return sha256(data).hexdigest()
   720→
   721→
   722→def build_batch_import_provenance(
   723→    *,
   724→    runner: str,
   725→    blind_packet_path: Path,
   726→    run_stamp: str,
   727→    batch_indexes: list[int],
   728→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   729→    """Build provenance payload used to trust assessment-bearing imports."""
   730→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_packet_path)
   731→    batch_indexes_1 = sorted({int(index) + 1 for index in batch_indexes})
   732→    return {
   733→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   734→        "blind": True,
   735→        "runner": runner,
   736→        "run_stamp": run_stamp,
   737→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   738→        "batch_count": len(batch_indexes_1),
   739→        "batch_indexes": batch_indexes_1,
   740→        "packet_path": str(blind_packet_path),
   741→        "packet_sha256": packet_hash,
   742→    }
   743→
   744→
   745→def selected_batch_indexes(
   746→    *,
   747→    raw_selection: str | None,
   748→    batch_count: int,
   749→    parse_fn,
   750→    colorize_fn,
   751→) -> list[int]:
   752→    """Validate selected batch indexes or exit with a CLI error."""
   753→    try:
   754→        selected = parse_fn(raw_selection, batch_count)
   755→    except ValueError as exc:
   756→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Error: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   757→        sys.exit(2)
   758→    if selected:
   759→        return selected
   760→    print(colorize_fn("  Error: no batches selected", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   761→    sys.exit(2)
   762→
   763→
   764→def prepare_run_artifacts(
   765→    *,
   766→    stamp: str,
   767→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   768→    batches: list[dict],
   769→    packet_path: Path,
   770→    run_root: Path,
   771→    repo_root: Path,
   772→    build_prompt_fn,
   773→    safe_write_text_fn,
   774→    colorize_fn,
   775→) -> tuple[Path, Path, dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path]]:
   776→    """Build prompt/output/log paths and persist prompts for selected batches."""
   777→    run_dir = run_root / stamp
   778→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   779→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   780→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   781→    prompts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   782→    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   783→    logs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   784→
   785→    selected_1_based = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]
   786→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Running holistic batches: {selected_1_based}", "bold"))
   787→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   788→
   789→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
```

```
675→def _build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
   676→    """Return a blind-review packet with score anchoring metadata removed."""
   677→    blind = deepcopy(packet)
   678→    for key in _BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS:
   679→        blind.pop(key, None)
   680→
   681→    config = blind.get("config")
   682→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   683→        sanitized = _sanitize_blind_config(config)
   684→        if sanitized:
   685→            blind["config"] = sanitized
   686→        else:
   687→            blind.pop("config", None)
   688→    return blind
   689→
   690→
   691→def build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
   692→    """Public wrapper for blind packet sanitization."""
   693→    return _build_blind_packet(packet)
   694→
   695→
   696→def _sanitize_blind_config(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   697→    """Drop score/target hints from config while preserving unrelated options."""
   698→    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
   699→    for key, value in config.items():
   700→        lowered = key.strip().lower()
   701→        if not lowered:
   702→            continue
   703→        if lowered in _BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS:
   704→            continue
   705→        if "target" in lowered:
   706→            continue
   707→        if lowered.endswith("_score"):
   708→            continue
   709→        sanitized[key] = value
   710→    return sanitized
   711→
   712→
   713→def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
   714→    """Compute sha256 hex digest for path contents (or None on read failure)."""
   715→    try:
   716→        data = path.read_bytes()
   717→    except OSError:
   718→        return None
   719→    return sha256(data).hexdigest()
   720→
   721→
   722→def build_batch_import_provenance(
   723→    *,
   724→    runner: str,
```

```
   186→def utc_now() -> str:
   187→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   188→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   189→
   190→
   191→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   192→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   193→    return {
   194→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   195→        "created": utc_now(),
   196→        "last_scan": None,
   197→        "scan_count": 0,
   198→        "overall_score": 0,
   199→        "objective_score": 0,
   200→        "strict_score": 0,
   201→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   202→        "stats": {},
   203→        "findings": {},
   204→        "scan_coverage": {},
   205→        "score_confidence": {},
   206→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   207→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   208→    }
   209→
   210→
   211→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   212→    try:
   213→        parsed = int(value)
   214→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215→        return default
   216→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   217→
   218→
   219→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   220→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   221→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   222→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   223→
   224→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   225→        state["findings"] = {}
   226→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   227→        state["stats"] = {}
   228→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   229→        state["scan_history"] = []
   230→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   231→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   232→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   233→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   234→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   235→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   236→
   237→    findings = state["findings"]
   238→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   239→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   240→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   241→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   242→            continue
   243→
   244→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
   245→        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   246→        finding.setdefault("file", "")
   247→        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
   248→        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   249→        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
   250→        finding.setdefault("detail", {})
   251→        finding.setdefault("status", "open")
   252→        finding["status"] = canonical_finding_status(
   253→            finding.get("status"),
   254→            default="open",
   255→        )
   256→        finding.setdefault("note", None)
   257→        finding.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   258→        finding.setdefault("last_seen", finding["first_seen"])
   259→        finding.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   260→        finding["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   261→            finding.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   262→        )
   263→        finding.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   264→        finding.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   265→        finding.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   266→
   267→    for finding_id in to_remove:
   268→        findings.pop(finding_id, None)
   269→
   270→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   271→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   272→            continue
   273→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   274→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   275→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   276→
   277→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   278→    return None
   279→
   280→
   281→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   282→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   283→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   284→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   285→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   286→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   287→
   288→    findings = state["findings"]
   289→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   290→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   291→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
   292→        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
   293→            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
   294→        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
   295→            raise ValueError(
   296→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
   297→            )
   298→
   299→        tier = finding.get("tier")
   300→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   301→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   302→
   303→        reopen_count = finding.get("reopen_count")
   304→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   305→            raise ValueError(
   306→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
   307→            )
   308→
   309→
   310→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
   311→    """JSON serializer that handles known types and rejects unknowns."""
   312→    if isinstance(obj, set):
   313→        return sorted(obj)
   314→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
   315→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
   316→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
   317→        return obj.isoformat()
   318→    raise TypeError(
   319→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def get_overall_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   324→    """Canonical overall score (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   325→    return state.get("overall_score")
   326→
   327→
   328→def get_objective_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   329→    """Canonical objective score (lenient, mechanical dimensions only)."""
   330→    return state.get("objective_score")
   331→
   332→
   333→def get_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   334→    """Canonical strict score (strict, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   335→    return state.get("strict_score")
   336→
   337→
   338→def get_verified_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   339→    """Strict score that only credits scan-verified fixes.
   340→
   341→    Returns None if no scan-verified score exists yet (fresh state or
   342→    no scan has run). Does not fall back to the unverified strict_score.
   343→    """
   344→    return state.get("verified_strict_score")
```

```
1→"""Finding upsert/auto-resolve helpers for scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import matched_ignore_pattern
     6→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import matches_exclusion
     7→
     8→
     9→def find_suspect_detectors(
    10→    existing: dict,
    11→    current_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    12→    force_resolve: bool,
    13→    ran_detectors: set[str] | None = None,
    14→) -> set[str]:
    15→    """Detectors that had open findings but likely did not actually run this scan."""
    16→    if force_resolve:
    17→        return set()
    18→
    19→    previous_open_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    20→    for finding in existing.values():
    21→        if finding["status"] != "open":
    22→            continue
    23→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    24→        previous_open_by_detector[detector] = (
    25→            previous_open_by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
    26→        )
    27→
    28→    # 'review' findings enter via `desloppify review --import`, not via scan phases.
    29→    # They are always marked suspect so the scan never auto-resolves them.
    30→    import_only_detectors = {"review"}
    31→    suspect: set[str] = set()
    32→
    33→    for detector, previous_count in previous_open_by_detector.items():
    34→        if detector in import_only_detectors:
    35→            suspect.add(detector)
    36→            continue
    37→        if current_by_detector.get(detector, 0) > 0:
    38→            continue
    39→        if ran_detectors is not None:
    40→            if detector not in ran_detectors:
    41→                suspect.add(detector)
    42→            continue
    43→        if previous_count >= 3:
    44→            suspect.add(detector)
    45→
    46→    return suspect
    47→
    48→
    49→def auto_resolve_disappeared(
    50→    existing: dict,
    51→    current_ids: set[str],
    52→    suspect_detectors: set[str],
    53→    now: str,
    54→    *,
    55→    lang: str | None,
    56→    scan_path: str | None,
    57→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    58→) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    59→    """Auto-resolve open/wontfix/fixed/false_positive findings absent from scan.
    60→
    61→    Returns (resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope).
    62→    """
    63→    resolved = skipped_other_lang = skipped_out_of_scope = 0
    64→
    65→    for finding_id, previous in existing.items():
    66→        if finding_id in current_ids or previous["status"] not in (
    67→            "open",
    68→            "wontfix",
    69→            "fixed",
    70→            "false_positive",
    71→        ):
    72→            continue
    73→
    74→        if lang and previous.get("lang") and previous["lang"] != lang:
    75→            skipped_other_lang += 1
    76→            continue
    77→
    78→        if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
    79→            prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    80→            if (
    81→                not previous["file"].startswith(prefix)
    82→                and previous["file"] != scan_path
    83→            ):
    84→                skipped_out_of_scope += 1
    85→                continue
    86→
    87→        if exclude and any(matches_exclusion(previous["file"], ex) for ex in exclude):
    88→            continue
    89→
    90→        if previous.get("detector", "unknown") in suspect_detectors:
    91→            continue
    92→
    93→        previous_status = previous["status"]
    94→        previous["status"] = "auto_resolved"
    95→        previous["resolved_at"] = now
    96→        previous["suppressed"] = False
    97→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
    98→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
    99→        previous["resolution_attestation"] = {
   100→            "kind": "scan_verified",
   101→            "text": "Disappeared from detector output",
   102→            "attested_at": now,
   103→            "scan_verified": True,
   104→        }
   105→        previous["note"] = (
   106→            "Fixed despite wontfix — disappeared from scan (was wontfix)"
   107→            if previous_status == "wontfix"
   108→            else "Disappeared from scan — likely fixed"
   109→        )
   110→        resolved += 1
   111→
   112→    return resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope
   113→
   114→
   115→def upsert_findings(
   116→    existing: dict,
   117→    current_findings: list[dict],
   118→    ignore: list[str],
   119→    now: str,
   120→    *,
   121→    lang: str | None,
   122→) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int]:
   123→    """Insert new findings and update existing ones.
   124→
   125→    Returns (current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count).
   126→    """
   127→    current_ids: set[str] = set()
   128→    new_count = reopened_count = ignored_count = 0
   129→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   130→
   131→    for finding in current_findings:
   132→        finding_id = finding["id"]
   133→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
   134→        current_ids.add(finding_id)
   135→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   136→        matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, finding["file"], ignore)
   137→        if matched_ignore:
   138→            ignored_count += 1
   139→
   140→        if lang:
   141→            finding["lang"] = lang
   142→
   143→        if finding_id not in existing:
   144→            existing[finding_id] = dict(finding)
   145→            if matched_ignore:
   146→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed"] = True
   147→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed_at"] = now
   148→                existing[finding_id]["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   149→                continue
   150→            new_count += 1
   151→            continue
   152→
   153→        previous = existing[finding_id]
   154→        previous.update(
   155→            last_seen=now,
   156→            tier=finding["tier"],
   157→            confidence=finding["confidence"],
   158→            summary=finding["summary"],
   159→            detail=finding.get("detail", {}),
   160→        )
   161→        if "zone" in finding:
   162→            previous["zone"] = finding["zone"]
   163→        if lang and not previous.get("lang"):
   164→            previous["lang"] = lang
   165→
   166→        if matched_ignore:
   167→            previous["suppressed"] = True
   168→            previous["suppressed_at"] = now
   169→            previous["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   170→            if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   171→                previous["status"] = "open"
   172→                previous["resolved_at"] = None
   173→                previous["note"] = (
   174→                    "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
   175→                )
   176→            continue
   177→
   178→        previous["suppressed"] = False
   179→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
   180→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   181→
   182→        if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved"):
   183→            previous_status = previous["status"]
   184→            previous["reopen_count"] = previous.get("reopen_count", 0) + 1
   185→            previous.pop("resolution_attestation", None)
   186→            previous.update(
   187→                status="open",
   188→                resolved_at=None,
   189→                note=(
   190→                    f"Reopened (×{previous['reopen_count']}) "
   191→                    f"— reappeared in scan (was {previous_status})"
   192→                ),
   193→            )
   194→            reopened_count += 1
   195→
   196→    return current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count
   197→
   198→
   199→__all__ = [
   200→    "auto_resolve_disappeared",
   201→    "find_suspect_detectors",
   202→    "upsert_findings",
   203→]
   204→
```

```
    27→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    28→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    29→    seen: set[str] = set()
    30→    ordered: list[str] = []
    31→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    32→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):
    33→            if key in seen:
    34→                continue
    35→            ordered.append(key)
    36→            seen.add(key)
    37→    return ",".join(ordered)
    38→
    39→
    40→def render_subjective_scores(entries: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    41→    return ", ".join(
    42→        f"{entry.get('name', 'Subjective')} {float(entry.get('strict', entry.get('score', 100.0))):.1f}%"
    43→        for entry in entries[:max_items]
    44→    )
    45→
    46→
    47→def render_subjective_names(entries: list[dict], *, max_names: int = 3) -> str:
    48→    count = len(entries)
    49→    names = ", ".join(
    50→        str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")) for entry in entries[:max_names]
    51→    )
    52→    if count > max_names:
    53→        names = f"{names}, +{count - max_names} more"
    54→    return names

    57→def coerce_notice_count(value: object) -> int:
    58→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    59→        return 0
    60→    if isinstance(value, int):
    61→        return value
    62→    if isinstance(value, float):
    63→        return int(value)
    64→    if isinstance(value, str):
    65→        try:
    66→            return int(value.strip())
    67→        except ValueError:
    68→            return 0
    69→    return 0
    70→
    71→
    72→def coerce_str_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    73→    if not isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):
    74→        return []
    75→    return [key for key in value if isinstance(key, str) and key]
    76→
    77→
    78→def subjective_rerun_command(

    96→        command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])

   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   105→def _subjective_display_name_from_key(dimension_key: str) -> str:
   106→    return scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(
   107→        dimension_key, dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
   108→    )
   109→
   110→
   111→def subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   112→    dimension_keys: list[str], entries: list[dict]
   113→) -> list[dict]:
   114→    by_key: dict[str, dict] = {}
   115→    for entry in entries:
   116→        for key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   117→            by_key.setdefault(str(key), entry)
   118→
   119→    mapped: list[dict] = []
   120→    for key in dimension_keys:
   121→        if key in by_key:
   122→            mapped.append(by_key[key])
   123→            continue
   124→        mapped.append(
   125→            {
   126→                "name": _subjective_display_name_from_key(key),
   127→                "score": 0.0,
   128→                "strict": 0.0,
   129→                "issues": 0,
   130→                "placeholder": False,
   131→                "cli_keys": [key],
   132→            }
   133→        )
   134→    return mapped
   135→
   136→
   137→def subjective_integrity_followup(
   138→    state: dict,
   139→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   140→    *,
   141→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   142→    max_items: int = 5,
   143→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   144→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   145→    raw_integrity_state = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   146→    integrity_state: dict[str, object] = (
   147→        raw_integrity_state if isinstance(raw_integrity_state, dict) else {}
   148→    )
   149→    status = str(integrity_state.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   150→    raw_target = integrity_state.get("target_score")
   151→    target_display = coerce_target_score(raw_target, fallback=threshold_value)
   152→    matched_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("matched_dimensions", []))
   153→    reset_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("reset_dimensions", []))
   154→
   155→    if status == "penalized" and reset_keys:
   156→        reset_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   157→            reset_keys, subjective_entries
   158→        )
   159→        return {
   160→            "status": "penalized",
   161→            "count": len(reset_keys),
   162→            "target": target_display,
   163→            "entries": reset_entries,
   164→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(reset_entries),
   165→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(reset_entries, max_items=max_items),
   166→        }
   167→
   168→    if status == "warn" and matched_keys:
   169→        matched_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   170→            matched_keys, subjective_entries
   171→        )
   172→        return {
   173→            "status": "warn",
   174→            "count": len(matched_keys),
   175→            "target": target_display,
   176→            "entries": matched_entries,
   177→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(matched_entries),
   178→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(matched_entries, max_items=max_items),
   179→        }
   180→
   181→    at_target = sorted(
   182→        [
   183→            entry
   184→            for entry in subjective_entries
   185→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   186→            and subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score(
   187→                float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   188→                threshold_value,
   189→            )
   190→        ],
   191→        key=lambda entry: str(entry.get("name", "")).lower(),
   192→    )
   193→    if not at_target:
   194→        return None
   195→
   196→    return {
   197→        "status": "at_target",
   198→        "count": len(at_target),
   199→        "target": threshold_value,
   200→        "entries": at_target,
   201→        "rendered": render_subjective_names(at_target),
   202→        "command": subjective_rerun_command(at_target, max_items=max_items),
   203→    }
   204→
   205→
   206→def subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   207→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None,
   208→    *,
   209→    fallback_target: float = 95.0,
   210→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   211→    if not integrity_notice:
   212→        return []
   213→
   214→    status = str(integrity_notice.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   215→    count = coerce_notice_count(integrity_notice.get("count", 0))
   216→    target_display = coerce_target_score(
   217→        integrity_notice.get("target"),
   218→        fallback=fallback_target,
   219→    )
   220→    rendered = str(integrity_notice.get("rendered", "subjective dimensions"))
   221→    command = str(integrity_notice.get("command", ""))
   222→
   223→    if status == "penalized":
   224→        return [
   225→            (
   226→                "red",
   227→                "WARNING: "
   228→                f"{count} subjective dimensions matched target {target_display:.1f} "
   229→                f"and were reset to 0.0 this scan: {rendered}.",
   230→            ),
   231→            (
   232→                "yellow",
   233→                "Anti-gaming safeguard applied. Re-review objectively and import fresh assessments.",
   234→            ),
   235→            ("dim", f"Rerun now: {command}"),
   236→        ]
   237→
   238→    if status == "warn":
   239→        dimension_label = "dimension is" if count == 1 else "dimensions are"
   240→        return [
   241→            (
   242→                "yellow",
   243→                "WARNING: "
   244→                f"{count} subjective {dimension_label} parked on target {target_display:.1f}. "
   245→                "Re-run that review with evidence-first scoring before treating this score as final.",
   246→            ),
   247→            ("dim", f"Next step: {command}"),
   248→        ]
   249→
   250→    if status == "at_target":
   251→        return [
   252→            (
   253→                "yellow",
   254→                "WARNING: "
   255→                f"{count} of your subjective scores matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   256→                f"Can you rerun them by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   257→            ),
   258→        ]
   259→
   260→    return []
   261→
   262→
   263→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   264→# Output-oriented helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_output)
   265→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   266→
   267→
   268→def build_subjective_followup(
   269→    state: dict,
   270→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   271→    *,
   272→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   273→    max_quality_items: int = 3,
   274→    max_integrity_items: int = 5,
   275→) -> SubjectiveFollowup:
   276→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   277→    threshold_label = f"{threshold_value:.1f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
   278→    low_assessed = sorted(
   279→        [
   280→            entry
   281→            for entry in subjective_entries
   282→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   283→            and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < threshold_value
   284→        ],
   285→        key=lambda entry: float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   286→    )
   287→    rendered = render_subjective_scores(low_assessed, max_items=max_quality_items)
   288→    command = subjective_rerun_command(
   289→        low_assessed,
   290→        max_items=max_quality_items,
   291→    )
   292→    integrity_notice = subjective_integrity_followup(
   293→        state,
   294→        subjective_entries,
   295→        threshold=threshold_value,
   296→        max_items=max_integrity_items,
   297→    )
   298→    integrity_lines = subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   299→        integrity_notice,
   300→        fallback_target=threshold_value,
   301→    )
   302→    return SubjectiveFollowup(
   303→        threshold=threshold_value,
   304→        threshold_label=threshold_label,
   305→        low_assessed=low_assessed,
   306→        rendered=rendered,
   307→        command=command,
   308→        integrity_notice=integrity_notice,
   309→        integrity_lines=integrity_lines,
   310→    )
   311→
   312→
   313→def show_subjective_paths(
   314→    state: dict,
   315→    dim_scores: dict,
   316→    *,
   317→    colorize_fn,
   318→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
   319→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   320→) -> None:
   321→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   322→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries_fn(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   323→    if not subjective_entries:
   324→        return
   325→
   326→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   327→        state,
   328→        subjective_entries,
   329→        threshold=threshold_value,
   330→        max_quality_items=3,
   331→        max_integrity_items=5,
   332→    )
   333→    unassessed = sorted(
   334→        [entry for entry in subjective_entries if entry["placeholder"]],
   335→        key=lambda item: item["name"].lower(),
   336→    )
   337→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   338→
   339→    all_findings = state.get("findings", {})
   340→    if not isinstance(all_findings, dict):
   341→        all_findings = {}
   342→    coverage_global, _reason_counts, _holistic_reasons = (
   343→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(all_findings)
   344→    )
   345→
   346→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   347→    if (
   348→        not unassessed
   349→        and not low_assessed
   350→        and stale_count == 0
   351→        and coverage_global <= 0
   352→        and not followup.integrity_notice
   353→    ):
   354→        return
   355→
   356→    # Integrity lines are always preserved (anti-gaming safeguard).
   357→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   358→        print(colorize_fn(f"    {message}", style))
   359→
   360→    # Collapsed summary replacing verbose Subjective path section.
   361→    parts: list[str] = []
   362→    if low_assessed:
   363→        parts.append(f"{len(low_assessed)} below target ({followup.threshold_label}%)")
   364→    if unassessed:
   365→        parts.append(f"{len(unassessed)} unassessed")
   366→    if stale_count:
   367→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   368→    if coverage_global > 0:
   369→        parts.append(f"{coverage_global} files need review")
   370→
   371→    if parts:
   372→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   373→        print(colorize_fn("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   374→
   375→    print()
   376→
   377→
   378→__all__ = [
   379→    "SubjectiveFollowup",
   380→    "build_subjective_followup",
   381→    "coerce_notice_count",
   382→    "coerce_str_keys",
```

```
    26→    if "design_coherence" in dim_set:
    27→        parts.append(

    81→        "Check whether each issue still exists in the current code. Do not re-report"
    82→        " issues that have been fixed or marked wontfix — focus on what remains or"
    83→        " what is new. If several past issues share a root cause, call that out."
    84→    )
    85→
    86→    for entry in issues:
    87→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    88→            continue
    89→        status = str(entry.get("status", "")).strip()
    90→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    91→        note = str(entry.get("note", "")).strip()
    92→
    93→        line = f"  - [{status}] {summary}"
    94→        if note:
    95→            line += f" (note: {note})"
    96→        lines.append(line)
    97→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
    98→
    99→
   100→def render_batch_prompt(
   101→    *,
   102→    repo_root: Path,
   103→    packet_path: Path,
   104→    batch_index: int,
   105→    batch: dict[str, object],
   106→) -> str:
   107→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   108→    name = str(batch.get("name", f"Batch {batch_index + 1}"))
   109→    dims_raw = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   110→    dims = (
   111→        [str(d) for d in dims_raw if isinstance(d, str) and d]
   112→        if isinstance(dims_raw, list | tuple)
   113→        else []
   114→    )
   115→    why = str(batch.get("why", "")).strip()
   116→    files_raw = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   117→    files = (
   118→        [str(f) for f in files_raw if isinstance(f, str) and f]
   119→        if isinstance(files_raw, list | tuple)
   120→        else []
   121→    )
   122→    findings_cap = max_batch_findings_for_dimension_count(len(dims))
   123→    file_lines = "\n".join(f"- {f}" for f in files) if files else "- (none)"
   124→    dim_text = ", ".join(dims) if dims else "(none)"
   125→    historical_focus = _render_historical_focus(batch)
   126→    dim_set = set(dims)
   127→    package_org_focus = ""
   128→    if "package_organization" in dim_set:
   129→        package_org_focus = (
   130→            "9a. For package_organization, ground scoring in objective structure signals from "
   131→            "`holistic_context.structure` (root_files fan_in/fan_out roles, directory_profiles, "
   132→            "coupling_matrix). Prefer thresholded evidence (for example: fan_in < 5 for root "
   133→            "stragglers, import-affinity > 60%, directories > 10 files with mixed concerns).\n"
   134→            "9b. Suggestions must include a staged reorg plan (target folders, move order, "
   135→            "and import-update/validation commands).\n"
   136→            "9c. Also consult `holistic_context.structure.flat_dir_findings` for directories "
   137→            "flagged as overloaded, fragmented, or thin-wrapper patterns.\n"
   138→        )
   139→    abstraction_focus = ""
   140→    if "abstraction_fitness" in dim_set:
   141→        abstraction_focus = (
   142→            "9d. For abstraction_fitness, use evidence from `holistic_context.abstractions`:\n"
   143→            "  - `delegation_heavy_classes`: classes where most methods forward to an inner "
   144→            "object — entries include class_name, delegate_target, sample_methods, and line number.\n"
   145→            "  - `facade_modules`: re-export-only modules with high re_export_ratio — entries "
   146→            "include samples (re-exported names) and loc.\n"
   147→            "  - `typed_dict_violations`: TypedDict fields accessed via .get()/.setdefault()/.pop() "
   148→            "— entries include typed_dict_name, violation_type, field, and line number.\n"
   149→            "  - `complexity_hotspots`: files where mechanical analysis found extreme parameter "
   150→            "counts, deep nesting, or disconnected responsibility clusters.\n"
   151→            "  Include `delegation_density`, `definition_directness`, and `type_discipline` "
   152→            "alongside existing sub-axes in dimension_notes when evidence supports it.\n"
   153→        )
   154→    scan_evidence_focus = _render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set)
   155→
   156→    scan_evidence_note = (
   157→        "Mechanical scan evidence: The blind packet contains `holistic_context.scan_evidence` "
   158→        "with aggregated signals from all mechanical detectors — including complexity hotspots, "
   159→        "error hotspots, signal density index (files flagged by multiple detectors), boundary "
   160→        "violations, and systemic patterns. Consult this section for investigative leads beyond "
   161→        "the seed files.\n\n"
   162→    )
   163→
   164→    return (
   165→        "You are a focused subagent reviewer for a single holistic investigation batch.\n\n"
   166→        f"Repository root: {repo_root}\n"
   167→        f"Blind packet: {packet_path}\n"
   168→        f"Batch index: {batch_index + 1}\n"
   169→        f"Batch name: {name}\n"
   170→        f"Batch dimensions: {dim_text}\n"
   171→        f"Batch rationale: {why}\n\n"
   172→        f"{scan_evidence_note}"
   173→        "Seed files (start here):\n"
   174→        f"{file_lines}\n\n"
   175→        f"{historical_focus}"
   176→        "Task requirements:\n"
   177→        "1. Read the blind packet and follow `system_prompt` constraints exactly.\n"
   178→        "1a. If previously flagged issues are listed above, use them as context for your review.\n"
   179→        "    Verify whether each still applies to the current code. Do not re-report fixed or\n"
   180→        "    wontfix issues. Use them as starting points to look deeper — inspect adjacent code\n"
   181→        "    and related modules for defects the prior review may have missed.\n"
   182→        "1c. Think structurally: when you spot multiple individual issues that share a common\n"
   183→        "    root cause (missing abstraction, duplicated pattern, inconsistent convention),\n"
   184→        "    explain the deeper structural issue in the finding, not just the surface symptom.\n"
   185→        "    If the pattern is significant enough, report the structural issue as its own finding\n"
   186→        "    with appropriate fix_scope ('multi_file_refactor' or 'architectural_change') and\n"
   187→        "    use `root_cause_cluster` to connect related symptom findings together.\n"
   188→        "2. Start with the seed files, then freely explore additional repository files likely to surface material issues.\n"
   189→        "2a. Prioritize high-signal leads: unexplored/lightly reviewed files, historical issue areas, and hotspot neighbors "
   190→        "(high coupling, god modules, large files, churn seams).\n"
   191→        "2b. Keep exploration targeted — follow strongest evidence paths first instead of attempting exhaustive coverage.\n"
   192→        "2c. Keep findings and scoring scoped to this batch's listed dimensions.\n"
   193→        "2d. Respect scope controls in the blind packet config: do not include files/directories marked by "
   194→        "`exclude`, `ignore`, or zone overrides that classify files as non-production (test/config/generated/vendor).\n"
   195→        f"3. Return 0-{findings_cap} high-quality findings for this batch (empty array allowed).\n"
   196→        "3a. Do not suppress real defects to keep scores high; report every material issue you can support with evidence.\n"
   197→        "3b. Do not default to 100. Reserve 100 for genuinely exemplary evidence in this batch.\n"
   198→        "4. Score/finding consistency is required: broader or more severe findings MUST lower dimension scores.\n"
   199→        f"4a. Any dimension scored below {LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include explicit feedback: add at least one "
   200→        "finding with the same `dimension` and a non-empty actionable `suggestion`.\n"
   201→        "5. Every finding must include `related_files` with at least 2 files when possible.\n"
   202→        "6. Every finding must include `dimension`, `identifier`, `summary`, `evidence`, `suggestion`, and `confidence`.\n"
   203→        "7. Every finding must include `impact_scope` and `fix_scope`.\n"
   204→        "8. Every scored dimension MUST include dimension_notes with concrete evidence.\n"
   205→        f"9. If a dimension score is >{HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f}, include `{DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY}` in dimension_notes.\n"
   206→        "10. Use exactly one decimal place for every assessment and abstraction sub-axis score.\n"
   207→        f"{package_org_focus}"
   208→        f"{abstraction_focus}"
   209→        f"{scan_evidence_focus}"
   210→        "11. Ignore prior chat context and any target-threshold assumptions.\n"
   211→        "12. Do not edit repository files.\n"
   212→        "13. Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences.\n\n"
   213→        "Scope enums:\n"
   214→        '- impact_scope: "local" | "module" | "subsystem" | "codebase"\n'
   215→        '- fix_scope: "single_edit" | "multi_file_refactor" | "architectural_change"\n\n'
   216→        "Output schema:\n"
   217→        "{\n"
   218→        f'  "batch": "{name}",\n'
   219→        f'  "batch_index": {batch_index + 1},\n'
   220→        '  "assessments": {"<dimension>": <0-100 with one decimal place>},\n'
   221→        '  "dimension_notes": {\n'
   222→        '    "<dimension>": {\n'
   223→        '      "evidence": ["specific code observations"],\n'
   224→        '      "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
   225→        '      "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
   226→        '      "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'

   228→        '      "sub_axes": {"abstraction_leverage": 0-100, "indirection_cost": 0-100, "interface_honesty": 0-100, "delegation_density": 0-100, "definition_directness": 0-100, "type_discipline": 0-100}  // required for abstraction_fitness when evidence supports it; all one decimal place\n'
   229→        "    }\n"
   230→        "  },\n"
   231→        '  "findings": [{\n'
   232→        '    "dimension": "<dimension>",\n'
   233→        '    "identifier": "short_id",\n'
   234→        '    "summary": "one-line defect summary",\n'
   235→        '    "related_files": ["relative/path.py"],\n'
```

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→
```

```
1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (

    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )

    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→

   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
```

```
168→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
   169→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
   170→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
   171→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
   172→        effort = "low"
   173→    return [
   174→        "codex",
   175→        "exec",
   176→        "--ephemeral",
   177→        "-C",
   178→        str(repo_root),
   179→        "-s",
   180→        "workspace-write",
   181→        "-c",
   182→        'approval_policy="never"',
   183→        "-c",
   184→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
   185→        "-o",
   186→        str(output_file),
   187→        prompt,
   188→    ]
   189→
   190→
   191→def _output_file_status_text(output_file: Path) -> str:
   192→    """Describe output file state for live log snapshots."""
   193→    if not output_file.exists():
   194→        return f"{output_file} (missing)"
   195→    try:
   196→        stat = output_file.stat()
   197→    except OSError as exc:
   198→        return f"{output_file} (exists; stat failed: {exc})"
   199→    modified_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime, tz=UTC).isoformat(
   200→        timespec="seconds"
   201→    )
   202→    return (
   203→        f"{output_file} (exists; bytes={stat.st_size}; "
   204→        f"modified={modified_at})"
   205→    )
   206→
   207→
```
