```
   767→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   768→    batches: list[dict],
   769→    packet_path: Path,
   770→    run_root: Path,
   771→    repo_root: Path,
   772→    build_prompt_fn,
   773→    safe_write_text_fn,
   774→    colorize_fn,
   775→) -> tuple[Path, Path, dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path]]:
   776→    """Build prompt/output/log paths and persist prompts for selected batches."""
   777→    run_dir = run_root / stamp
   778→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   779→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   780→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   781→    prompts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   782→    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   783→    logs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   784→
   785→    selected_1_based = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]
   786→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Running holistic batches: {selected_1_based}", "bold"))
   787→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   788→
   789→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   790→    output_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   791→    log_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   792→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   793→        batch = batches[idx] if isinstance(batches[idx], dict) else {}
   794→        prompt_text = build_prompt_fn(
   795→            repo_root=repo_root,
   796→            packet_path=packet_path,
   797→            batch_index=idx,
   798→            batch=batch,
   799→        )
   800→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.md"
   801→        output_file = results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   802→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   803→        safe_write_text_fn(prompt_file, prompt_text)
   804→        prompt_files[idx] = prompt_file
   805→        output_files[idx] = output_file
   806→        log_files[idx] = log_file
   807→    return run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files
   808→
   809→
   810→def _emit_progress(
   811→    progress_fn,
   812→    batch_index: int,
   813→    event: str,
   814→    code: int | None = None,
   815→    *,
   816→    details: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   817→) -> Exception | None:
   818→    """Forward a progress event and return callback exceptions to caller."""
   819→    if not callable(progress_fn):
   820→        return None
   821→    payload = dict(details or {})
   822→    progress_event = BatchProgressEvent(
   823→        batch_index=batch_index,
   824→        event=event,
   825→        code=code,
   826→        details=payload,
   827→    )
   828→    try:
   829→        progress_fn(progress_event)
   830→        return None
   831→    except TypeError as exc:
   832→        if not _looks_like_progress_signature_mismatch(exc):
   833→            return RuntimeError(
   834→                f"progress callback failed for event={event} batch={batch_index}: {exc}"
   835→            )
   836→        try:
   837→            progress_fn(batch_index, event, code, **payload)
   838→            return None
   839→        except Exception as legacy_exc:
   840→            return RuntimeError(
   841→                f"progress callback failed for event={event} batch={batch_index}: {legacy_exc}"
   842→            )
   843→    except Exception as exc:
   844→        return RuntimeError(
   845→            f"progress callback failed for event={event} batch={batch_index}: {exc}"
   846→        )
   847→
   848→
   849→def _record_execution_error(
   850→    *,
   851→    error_log_fn,
   852→    failures: set[int],
   853→    idx: int,
   854→    exc: Exception,
   855→) -> None:
   856→    """Record an execution/progress error through shared failure plumbing."""
   857→    if callable(error_log_fn):
   858→        try:
   859→            error_log_fn(idx, exc)
   860→        except Exception:
   861→            pass
   862→    failures.add(idx)
   863→
```
```

    51→def merge_scan(

    54→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,

    99→    )

   135→        for finding in existing.values()
   136→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
   137→    ]
   138→
   139→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   140→    return _build_merge_diff(
   141→        new_count=new_count,
   142→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   143→        reopened_count=reopened_count,
   144→        current_ids=current_ids,

   150→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   151→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   152→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   153→    )
```

```
1→"""Batch execution flow helpers for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import math
     8→import shlex
     9→import sys
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    12→from pathlib import Path
    13→from collections.abc import Callable
    14→
    15→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import (
    16→    coerce_non_negative_float,
    17→    coerce_positive_float,
    18→    coerce_positive_int,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure, warn_best_effort
    21→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (
    22→    LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY,
    23→    REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import load_dimensions_for_lang
    26→
    27→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    28→_RUN_LOG_WARNED_PATHS: set[Path] = set()
    29→
    30→
    31→def _append_run_log(run_log_path: Path, message: str) -> None:
    32→    """Append a timestamped message to the run log file."""
    33→    line = f"{datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec='seconds')} {message}\n"
    34→    try:
    35→        with run_log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    36→            handle.write(line)
    37→    except OSError as exc:
    38→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"append review run log {run_log_path}", exc)
    39→        if run_log_path not in _RUN_LOG_WARNED_PATHS:
    40→            _RUN_LOG_WARNED_PATHS.add(run_log_path)
    41→            warn_best_effort(
    42→                f"Could not append review run log at {run_log_path}: {exc}"
    43→            )
    44→        return
    45→
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class _ProgressCtx:
    49→    """Shared mutable state for the batch progress reporter."""
    50→
    51→    run_log_path: Path
    52→    colorize_fn: Callable[[str, str], str]
    53→    total_batches: int
    54→    batch_positions: dict[int, int]
    55→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]]
    56→    stall_warning_seconds: int
    57→    stall_warned_batches: set[int]
    58→
    59→
    60→def _report_progress(
    61→    ctx: _ProgressCtx,
    62→    batch_index: int | object,
    63→    event: str | None = None,
    64→    code: int | None = None,
    65→    **details,
    66→) -> None:
    67→    """Handle batch progress events (heartbeat, queued, start, done)."""
    68→    if event is None and hasattr(batch_index, "event"):
    69→        progress_event = batch_index
    70→        event = str(getattr(progress_event, "event", "")).strip()
    71→        code = getattr(progress_event, "code", code)
    72→        details_obj = getattr(progress_event, "details", {})
    73→        details = details_obj if isinstance(details_obj, dict) else {}
    74→        batch_index = int(getattr(progress_event, "batch_index", -1))
    75→    else:
    76→        batch_index = int(batch_index) if isinstance(batch_index, int | float) else -1
    77→
    78→    if not event:
    79→        return
    80→
    81→    if event == "heartbeat":
    82→        _handle_heartbeat_event(ctx, details)
    83→        return
    84→
    85→    position = ctx.batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
    86→    key = str(batch_index + 1)
    87→    state = ctx.batch_status.setdefault(
    88→        key, {"position": position, "status": "pending"},
    89→    )
    90→    if event == "queued":
    91→        state["status"] = "queued"
    92→        print(ctx.colorize_fn(f"  Batch {position}/{ctx.total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})", "dim"))
    93→        _append_run_log(ctx.run_log_path, f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{ctx.total_batches}")
    94→        return
    95→    if event == "start":
    96→        state["status"] = "running"
    97→        state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    98→        print(ctx.colorize_fn(f"  Batch {position}/{ctx.total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})", "dim"))
    99→        _append_run_log(ctx.run_log_path, f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{ctx.total_batches}")
   100→        return
   101→    if event == "done":
   102→        _handle_done_event(ctx, batch_index, position, state, code, details)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _handle_heartbeat_event(ctx: _ProgressCtx, details: dict) -> None:
   106→    """Process a heartbeat progress event."""
   107→    active = details.get("active_batches")
   108→    queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   109→    elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   110→    if not isinstance(active, list):
   111→        active = []
   112→    if not isinstance(queued, list):
   113→        queued = []
   114→    if not active and not queued:
   115→        return
   116→    segments: list[str] = []
   117→    for idx in active[:6]:
   118→        secs = 0
   119→        if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   120→            raw_secs = elapsed.get(idx, 0)
   121→            secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   122→        segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   123→    if len(active) > 6:
   124→        segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   125→    queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}" if queued else ""
   126→    print(
   127→        ctx.colorize_fn(
   128→            f"  Batch heartbeat: {len(active)}/{ctx.total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   129→            f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   130→            "dim",
   131→        )
   132→    )
   133→    _append_run_log(
   134→        ctx.run_log_path,
   135→        "heartbeat "
   136→        f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   137→        f"elapsed={{{', '.join(f'{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}' for idx in active)}}}",
   138→    )
   139→    if ctx.stall_warning_seconds > 0 and isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   140→        slow_active = [
   141→            idx for idx in active
   142→            if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   143→            and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= ctx.stall_warning_seconds
   144→        ]
   145→        newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in ctx.stall_warned_batches]
   146→        if newly_warned:
   147→            ctx.stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   148→            print(
   149→                ctx.colorize_fn(
   150→                    f"  Stall warning: batches {[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   151→                    f"{ctx.stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   152→                    "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs.",
   153→                    "yellow",
   154→                )
   155→            )
   156→            _append_run_log(
   157→                ctx.run_log_path,
   158→                f"stall-warning threshold={ctx.stall_warning_seconds}s "
   159→                f"batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}",
   160→            )
   161→
   162→
   163→def _handle_done_event(
   164→    ctx: _ProgressCtx,
   165→    batch_index: int,
   166→    position: int,
   167→    state: dict[str, object],
   168→    code: int | None,
   169→    details: dict,
   170→) -> None:
   171→    """Process a batch-done progress event."""
   172→    status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   173→    tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   174→    elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   175→    elapsed_suffix = ""
   176→    if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   177→        elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   178→        state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   179→    state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   180→    state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   181→    state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   182→    if batch_index in ctx.stall_warned_batches:
   183→        ctx.stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   184→    print(
   185→        ctx.colorize_fn(
   186→            f"  Batch {position}/{ctx.total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   187→            tone,
   188→        )
   189→    )
   190→    _append_run_log(
   191→        ctx.run_log_path,
   192→        f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{ctx.total_batches} "
   193→        f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}",
   194→    )
   195→
   196→
   197→def _validate_runner(runner: str, *, colorize_fn) -> None:
   198→    """Validate review batch runner."""
   199→    if runner == "codex":
   200→        return
   201→    print(
   202→        colorize_fn(
   203→            f"  Error: unsupported runner '{runner}' (supported: codex)", "red"
   204→        ),
   205→        file=sys.stderr,
   206→    )
   207→    sys.exit(2)
   208→
   209→
   210→def _require_batches(
   211→    packet: dict,
   212→    *,
   213→    colorize_fn,
   214→    suggested_prepare_cmd: str | None = None,
   215→) -> list[dict]:
   216→    """Return investigation batches or exit with a clear error."""
   217→    batches = packet.get("investigation_batches", [])
   218→    if isinstance(batches, list) and batches:
   219→        return batches
   220→    print(
   221→        colorize_fn("  Error: packet has no investigation_batches.", "red"),
   222→        file=sys.stderr,
   223→    )
   224→    if isinstance(suggested_prepare_cmd, str) and suggested_prepare_cmd.strip():
   225→        print(
   226→            colorize_fn(
   227→                f"  Regenerate review context first: `{suggested_prepare_cmd}`",
   228→                "yellow",
   229→            ),
   230→            file=sys.stderr,
   231→        )
   232→    print(
   233→        colorize_fn(
   234→            "  Happy path: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`.",
   235→            "dim",
   236→        ),
   237→        file=sys.stderr,
   238→    )
   239→    sys.exit(1)
   240→
   241→
   242→def _print_review_quality(quality: object, *, colorize_fn) -> None:
   243→    """Render merged review quality summary when present."""
   244→    if not isinstance(quality, dict):
   245→        return
   246→    coverage = quality.get("dimension_coverage")
   247→    density = quality.get("evidence_density")
   248→    high_missing_issue_note = quality.get(
   249→        REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
   250→    )
   251→    if not isinstance(high_missing_issue_note, int | float):
   252→        high_missing_issue_note = quality.get(
   253→            LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
   254→        )
   255→    finding_pressure = quality.get("finding_pressure")
   256→    dims_with_findings = quality.get("dimensions_with_findings")
   257→    if not isinstance(coverage, int | float) or not isinstance(density, int | float):
   258→        return
   259→
   260→    pressure_segment = ""
   261→    if isinstance(finding_pressure, int | float) and isinstance(dims_with_findings, int):
   262→        pressure_segment = (
   263→            f", finding-pressure {float(finding_pressure):.2f} "
   264→            f"across {dims_with_findings} dims"
   265→        )
   266→    print(
   267→        colorize_fn(
   268→            "  Review quality: "
   269→            f"dimension coverage {float(coverage):.2f}, "
   270→            f"evidence density {float(density):.2f}, "
   271→            f"high-score-missing-issue-note {int(high_missing_issue_note or 0)}"
   272→            f"{pressure_segment}",
   273→            "dim",
   274→        )
   275→    )
   276→
   277→
   278→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   279→    *,
   280→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   281→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   282→) -> list[str]:
   283→    """Collect normalized file paths reviewed in the selected batch set."""
   284→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   285→    seen: set[str] = set()
   286→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   287→        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(batches):
   288→            continue
   289→        batch = batches[idx]
   290→        files = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   291→        if not isinstance(files, list):
   292→            continue
   293→        for raw in files:
   294→            if not isinstance(raw, str):
   295→                continue
   296→            path = raw.strip().strip(",'\"")
   297→            if not path or path in {".", ".."}:
   298→                continue
   299→            if path.endswith("/"):
   300→                continue
   301→            if path in seen:
   302→                continue
   303→            seen.add(path)
   304→            reviewed.append(path)
   305→    return reviewed
   306→
   307→
   308→def _normalize_dimension_list(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   309→    """Normalize dimension collections to a stable, de-duplicated list."""
   310→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   311→        return []
   312→    out: list[str] = []
   313→    seen: set[str] = set()
   314→    for item in raw:
   315→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   316→            continue
   317→        dim = item.strip()
   318→        if not dim or dim in seen:
   319→            continue
   320→        seen.add(dim)
   321→        out.append(dim)
   322→    return out
   323→
   324→
   325→def _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name: str) -> list[str]:
   326→    """Return default scored subjective dimensions for one language."""
   327→    try:
   328→        default_dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   329→    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
   330→        return []
   331→    return _normalize_dimension_list(default_dims)
   332→
   333→
   334→def _missing_scored_dimensions(
   335→    *,
   336→    selected_dims: list[str],
   337→    scored_dims: list[str],
   338→) -> list[str]:
   339→    selected = set(selected_dims)
   340→    return [dim for dim in scored_dims if dim not in selected]
   341→
   342→
   343→def _missing_dimensions_command(*, missing_dims: list[str], scan_path: str) -> str:
   344→    """Return rerun command for missing subjective dimensions."""
   345→    base = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import"
   346→    if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
   347→        base += f" --path {shlex.quote(scan_path)}"
   348→    if missing_dims:
   349→        base += f" --dimensions {','.join(missing_dims)}"
   350→    return base
   351→
   352→
   353→def _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   354→    *,
   355→    selected_dims: list[str],
   356→    scored_dims: list[str],
   357→    explicit_selection: bool,
   358→    scan_path: str,
   359→    colorize_fn,
   360→) -> None:
   361→    """Print trigger-time notice when run scope is a scored-dimension subset."""
   362→    if not scored_dims:
   363→        return
   364→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   365→        selected_dims=selected_dims,
   366→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   367→    )
   368→    if not missing_dims:
   369→        return
   370→
   371→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in selected_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   372→    scope_reason = (
   373→        "explicit --dimensions selection"
   374→        if explicit_selection
   375→        else "language default review dimension set"
   376→    )
   377→    tone = "yellow" if explicit_selection else "red"
   378→    print(
   379→        colorize_fn(
   380→            "  WARNING: this run targets "
   381→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions "
   382→            f"({scope_reason}).",
   383→            tone,
   384→        )
   385→    )
   386→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   387→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   388→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   389→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Missing from this run: {preview}", "yellow"))
   390→    print(
   391→        colorize_fn(
   392→            "  Rerun missing dimensions: "
   393→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   394→            "dim",
   395→        )
   396→    )
   397→
   398→
   399→def _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   400→    *,
   401→    assessed_dims: list[str],
   402→    scored_dims: list[str],
   403→    scan_path: str,
   404→    colorize_fn,
   405→) -> list[str]:
   406→    """Print result-time notice when merged import covers only a subset."""
   407→    if not scored_dims:
   408→        return []
   409→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   410→        selected_dims=assessed_dims,
   411→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   412→    )
   413→    if not missing_dims:
   414→        return []
   415→
   416→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in assessed_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   417→    print(
   418→        colorize_fn(
   419→            "  Coverage gap: imported assessments for "
   420→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions.",
   421→            "yellow",
   422→        )
   423→    )
   424→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   425→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   426→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   427→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Still missing: {preview}", "yellow"))
   428→    print(
   429→        colorize_fn(
   430→            "  Run to cover missing dimensions: "
   431→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   432→            "dim",
   433→        )
   434→    )
   435→    return missing_dims
   436→
   437→
   438→def do_run_batches(
   439→    args,
   440→    state,
   441→    lang,
   442→    state_file,
   443→    *,
   444→    config: dict | None,
   445→    run_stamp_fn,
   446→    load_or_prepare_packet_fn,
   447→    selected_batch_indexes_fn,
   448→    prepare_run_artifacts_fn,
   449→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   450→    execute_batches_fn,
   451→    collect_batch_results_fn,
   452→    print_failures_fn,
   453→    print_failures_and_exit_fn,
   454→    merge_batch_results_fn,
   455→    build_import_provenance_fn,
   456→    do_import_fn,
   457→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   458→    safe_write_text_fn,
   459→    colorize_fn,
   460→    project_root: Path,
   461→    subagent_runs_dir: Path,
   462→) -> None:
   463→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   464→    config = config or {}
   465→    runner = getattr(args, "runner", "codex")
   466→    _validate_runner(runner, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   467→    allow_partial = bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False))
   468→    run_parallel = bool(getattr(args, "parallel", False))
   469→    max_parallel_batches = coerce_positive_int(
   470→        getattr(args, "max_parallel_batches", None),
   471→        default=3,
   472→        minimum=1,
   473→    )
   474→    heartbeat_seconds = coerce_positive_float(
   475→        getattr(args, "batch_heartbeat_seconds", None),
   476→        default=15.0,
   477→        minimum=0.1,
   478→    )
   479→    batch_timeout_seconds = coerce_positive_int(
   480→        getattr(args, "batch_timeout_seconds", None),
   481→        default=2 * 60 * 60,
   482→        minimum=1,
   483→    )
   484→    batch_max_retries = coerce_positive_int(
   485→        getattr(args, "batch_max_retries", None),
   486→        default=1,
   487→        minimum=0,
   488→    )
   489→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = coerce_non_negative_float(
   490→        getattr(args, "batch_retry_backoff_seconds", None),
   491→        default=2.0,
   492→    )
   493→    stall_warning_seconds = coerce_positive_int(
   494→        getattr(args, "batch_stall_warning_seconds", None),
   495→        default=0,
   496→        minimum=0,
   497→    )
   498→    stall_kill_seconds = coerce_positive_int(
   499→        getattr(args, "batch_stall_kill_seconds", None),
   500→        default=0,
   501→        minimum=0,
   502→    )
   503→
   504→    stamp = run_stamp_fn()
   505→    packet, immutable_packet_path, prompt_packet_path = load_or_prepare_packet_fn(
   506→        args,
   507→        state=state,
   508→        lang=lang,
   509→        config=config,
   510→        stamp=stamp,
   511→    )
   512→
   513→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   514→    packet_dimensions = _normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   515→    scored_dimensions = _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   516→    _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   517→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
   518→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   519→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
   520→        scan_path=scan_path,
   521→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   522→    )
   523→    suggested_prepare_cmd = f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
   524→    batches = _require_batches(
   525→        packet,
   526→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   527→        suggested_prepare_cmd=suggested_prepare_cmd,
   528→    )
   529→
   530→    selected_indexes = selected_batch_indexes_fn(args, batch_count=len(batches))
   531→    total_batches = len(selected_indexes)
   532→    effective_workers = min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches) if run_parallel else 1
   533→    waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
   534→    worst_case_seconds = waves * batch_timeout_seconds
   535→    worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil(worst_case_seconds / 60))
   536→    print(
   537→        colorize_fn(
   538→            "  Runtime expectation: "
   539→            f"{total_batches} batch(es), workers={effective_workers}, "
   540→            f"timeout-per-batch={int(batch_timeout_seconds / 60)}m, "
   541→            f"worst-case upper bound ~{worst_case_minutes}m.",
   542→            "dim",
   543→        )
   544→    )
   545→    run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files = prepare_run_artifacts_fn(
   546→        stamp=stamp,
   547→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   548→        batches=batches,
   549→        packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   550→        run_root=subagent_runs_dir,
   551→        repo_root=project_root,
   552→    )
   553→    raw_run_log_file = getattr(args, "run_log_file", None)
   554→    run_log_file_value = (
   555→        raw_run_log_file.strip()
   556→        if isinstance(raw_run_log_file, str) and raw_run_log_file.strip()
   557→        else None
   558→    )
   559→    if run_log_file_value is not None:
   560→        candidate = Path(run_log_file_value).expanduser()
   561→        run_log_path = candidate if candidate.is_absolute() else project_root / candidate
   562→    else:
   563→        run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   564→    run_log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   565→    _append_run_log(run_log_path,
   566→        "run-start "
   567→        f"runner={runner} parallel={run_parallel} max_parallel={max_parallel_batches} "
   568→        f"timeout={batch_timeout_seconds}s heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s "
   569→        f"stall_warning={stall_warning_seconds}s stall_kill={stall_kill_seconds}s "
   570→        f"retries={batch_max_retries} "
   571→        f"retry_backoff={batch_retry_backoff_seconds:.1f}s upper_bound={worst_case_minutes}m "
   572→        f"selected={[idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]}"
   573→    )
   574→    _append_run_log(run_log_path, f"run-path {run_dir}")
   575→    _append_run_log(run_log_path, f"packet {immutable_packet_path}")
   576→    _append_run_log(run_log_path, f"blind-packet {prompt_packet_path}")
   577→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Live run log: {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   578→
   579→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   580→        print(
   581→            colorize_fn(
   582→                "  Dry run only: prompts generated, runner execution skipped.", "yellow"
   583→            )
   584→        )
   585→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Immutable packet: {immutable_packet_path}", "dim"))
   586→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Blind packet: {prompt_packet_path}", "dim"))
   587→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Prompts: {run_dir / 'prompts'}", "dim"))
   588→        _append_run_log(run_log_path, "run-finished dry-run")
   589→        return
   590→
   591→    batch_positions = {batch_idx: pos + 1 for pos, batch_idx in enumerate(selected_indexes)}
   592→    summary_created_at = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   593→    progress_ctx = _ProgressCtx(
   594→        run_log_path=run_log_path,
   595→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   596→        total_batches=total_batches,
   597→        batch_positions=batch_positions,
   598→        batch_status={
   599→            str(idx + 1): {
   600→                "position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0),
   601→                "status": "pending",
   602→                "prompt_path": str(prompt_files[idx]),
   603→                "result_path": str(output_files[idx]),
   604→                "log_path": str(log_files[idx]),
   605→            }
   606→            for idx in selected_indexes
   607→        },
   608→        stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
   609→        stall_warned_batches=set(),
   610→    )
   611→
   612→    if run_parallel:
   613→        print(
   614→            colorize_fn(
   615→                "  Parallel runner config: "
   616→                f"max-workers={min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches)}, "
   617→                f"heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   618→                "dim",
   619→            )
   620→        )
   621→
   622→    from functools import partial
   623→    bound_progress = partial(_report_progress, progress_ctx)
   624→    run_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   625→
   626→    def _write_run_summary(
   627→        *,
   628→        successful_indexes: list[int],
   629→        failure_set: set[int],
   630→        interrupted: bool = False,
   631→        interruption_reason: str | None = None,
   632→    ) -> dict[str, object]:
   633→        payload: dict[str, object] = {
   634→            "created_at": summary_created_at,
   635→            "run_stamp": stamp,
   636→            "runner": runner,
   637→            "parallel": run_parallel,
   638→            "selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes],
   639→            "successful_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   640→            "failed_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   641→            "allow_partial": allow_partial,
   642→            "max_parallel_batches": max_parallel_batches if run_parallel else 1,
   643→            "batch_timeout_seconds": batch_timeout_seconds,
   644→            "batch_max_retries": batch_max_retries,
   645→            "batch_retry_backoff_seconds": batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   646→            "batch_heartbeat_seconds": heartbeat_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   647→            "batch_stall_warning_seconds": stall_warning_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   648→            "batch_stall_kill_seconds": stall_kill_seconds,
   649→            "immutable_packet": str(immutable_packet_path),
   650→            "blind_packet": str(prompt_packet_path),
   651→            "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   652→            "logs_dir": str(logs_dir),
   653→            "run_log": str(run_log_path),
   654→            "batches": progress_ctx.batch_status,
   655→        }
   656→        if interrupted:
   657→            payload["interrupted"] = True
   658→            if isinstance(interruption_reason, str) and interruption_reason.strip():
   659→                payload["interruption_reason"] = interruption_reason
   660→        safe_write_text_fn(run_summary_path, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   661→        return payload
   662→
   663→    def _mark_inflight_batches_interrupted() -> None:
   664→        completed_at = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   665→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   666→            key = str(idx + 1)
   667→            state = progress_ctx.batch_status.setdefault(
   668→                key,
   669→                {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   670→            )
   671→            status = str(state.get("status", "pending"))
   672→            if status in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   673→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   674→                state["completed_at"] = completed_at
   675→
   676→    # Persist a baseline summary early so interrupted runs still leave usable metadata.
   677→    _write_run_summary(successful_indexes=[], failure_set=set())
   678→
   679→    def _make_task(idx):
   680→        def task():
   681→            return run_codex_batch_fn(
   682→                repo_root=project_root,

   737→        )

   740→    allowed_dims = {
```

```
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})

    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )

   163→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   164→    dim_scores: dict,
   165→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   166→) -> str | None:
   167→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   168→    lowest_name = None
   169→    lowest_score = 101.0
   170→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   171→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   172→        if not ds:
   173→            continue
   174→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   175→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   176→            lowest_score = strict_val
   177→            lowest_name = dim.name
   178→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   179→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   180→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   181→            lowest_score = strict_val
   182→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   183→    return lowest_name
   184→
   185→
   186→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   187→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   188→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   189→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   190→        return {}
   191→
   192→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   193→    for finding in findings.values():
   194→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   195→            continue
   196→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   197→            continue
   198→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   199→        dimension = ""
   200→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   201→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   202→        if not dimension:
   203→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   204→        if not dimension:
   205→            continue
   206→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   207→    return counts
   208→

   212→) -> None:

   229→        )
```

```
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(

    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""

    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low

    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)

    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
```

```
    42→def _lookup_from_last_query(number: int) -> str | None:
    43→    """Look up finding ID by 1-based index from the last query output."""
    44→    try:
    45→        qf = query_file_path()
    46→        if not qf.exists():
    47→            return None
    48→        data = json.loads(qf.read_text())
    49→        items = data.get("items") or data.get("findings") or []
    50→        if not items:
    51→            return None
    52→        if number < 1 or number > len(items):
    53→            return None
    54→        item = items[number - 1]
    55→        return item.get("id") or item.get("finding_id") or None
    56→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError, KeyError, TypeError):
    57→        return None
    58→
    59→

    99→            )
   100→            return

   130→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
```

```
1→"""Concern generators — mechanical findings → subjective review bridge.
     2→
     3→Concerns are ephemeral: computed on-demand from current state, never persisted.
     4→Only LLM-confirmed concerns become persistent Finding objects via review import.
     5→
     6→Generators focus on cross-cutting synthesis — bundling all signals per file so
     7→the LLM gets a complete picture, and surfacing systemic patterns across files
     8→that no single detector captures.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from collections import defaultdict
    15→from dataclasses import dataclass
    16→from typing import Any, Literal, TypedDict, cast
    17→
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class Concern:
    23→    """A potential design problem surfaced by mechanical signals."""
    24→
    25→    type: str  # concern classification
    26→    file: str  # primary file (relative path)
    27→    summary: str  # human-readable 1-liner
    28→    evidence: tuple[str, ...]  # supporting data points
    29→    question: str  # specific question for LLM to evaluate
    30→    fingerprint: str  # stable hash for dismissal tracking
    31→    source_findings: tuple[str, ...]  # finding IDs that triggered this
    32→
    33→
    34→class ConcernSignals(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    """Typed signal payload extracted from mechanical findings."""
    36→
    37→    max_params: float
    38→    max_nesting: float
    39→    loc: float
    40→    function_count: float
    41→    monster_loc: float
    42→    monster_funcs: list[str]
    43→
    44→
    45→_NUMERIC_SIGNAL_KEYS = ("max_params", "max_nesting", "loc", "function_count")
    46→SignalKey = Literal["max_params", "max_nesting", "loc", "function_count", "monster_loc"]
    47→
    48→
    49→def _update_max_signal(signals: ConcernSignals, key: SignalKey, value: object) -> None:
    50→    """Update numeric signal key with max(existing, value) when value is valid."""
    51→    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int | float) or value <= 0:
    52→        return
    53→    current = float(signals.get(key, 0.0))
    54→    signals[key] = max(current, float(value))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _fingerprint(concern_type: str, file: str, key_signals: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
    58→    """Stable hash of (type, file, sorted key signals)."""
    59→    raw = f"{concern_type}::{file}::{','.join(sorted(key_signals))}"
    60→    return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
    61→
    62→
    63→def _is_dismissed(
    64→    dismissals: dict, fingerprint: str, source_finding_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    65→) -> bool:
    66→    """Check if a concern was previously dismissed and source findings unchanged."""
    67→    entry = dismissals.get(fingerprint)
    68→    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    69→        return False
    70→    prev_sources = set(entry.get("source_finding_ids", []))
    71→    return prev_sources == set(source_finding_ids)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _open_findings(state: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    75→    """Return all open findings from state."""
    76→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    77→    return [
    78→        f for f in findings.values()
    79→        if isinstance(f, dict) and f.get("status") == "open"
    80→    ]
    81→
    82→
    83→def _group_by_file(state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    84→    """Group open findings by file, excluding holistic (file='.')."""
    85→    by_file: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    86→    for f in _open_findings(state):
    87→        file = f.get("file", "")
    88→        if file and file != ".":
    89→            by_file[file].append(f)
    90→    return dict(by_file)
    91→
    92→
    93→# ── Signal extraction ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    94→
    95→
    96→def _extract_signals(findings: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> ConcernSignals:
    97→    """Extract key quantitative signals from a file's findings."""
    98→    signals: ConcernSignals = {}
    99→    monster_funcs: list[str] = []
   100→
   101→    for f in findings:
   102→        det = f.get("detector", "")
   103→        detail_raw = f.get("detail", {})
   104→        detail = detail_raw if isinstance(detail_raw, dict) else {}
   105→
   106→        if det == "structural":
   107→            s = detail.get("signals", {})
   108→            if isinstance(s, dict):
   109→                for key in _NUMERIC_SIGNAL_KEYS:
   110→                    _update_max_signal(signals, cast(SignalKey, key), s.get(key, 0))
   111→
   112→        if det == "smells" and detail.get("smell_id") == "monster_function":
   113→            _update_max_signal(signals, "monster_loc", detail.get("loc", 0))
   114→            func = detail.get("function", "")
   115→            if isinstance(func, str) and func:
   116→                monster_funcs.append(func)
   117→
   118→    if monster_funcs:
   119→        signals["monster_funcs"] = monster_funcs
   120→    return signals
   121→
   122→
   123→def _has_elevated_signals(findings: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:
   124→    """Does any finding have signals strong enough to flag on its own?"""
   125→    for f in findings:
   126→        det = f.get("detector", "")
   127→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
   128→
   129→        if det == "structural":
   130→            s = detail.get("signals", {})
   131→            if isinstance(s, dict):
   132→                if s.get("max_params", 0) >= 8:
   133→                    return True
   134→                if s.get("max_nesting", 0) >= 6:
   135→                    return True
   136→                if s.get("loc", 0) >= 300:
   137→                    return True
   138→
   139→        if det == "smells" and detail.get("smell_id") == "monster_function":
   140→            return True
   141→
   142→        if det in ("dupes", "boilerplate_duplication", "coupling",
   143→                    "responsibility_cohesion"):
   144→            return True
   145→
   146→    return False
   147→
   148→
   149→# ── Concern classification ───────────────────────────────────────────
   150→
```

```
    27→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    28→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    29→    seen: set[str] = set()
    30→    ordered: list[str] = []
    31→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    32→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):
    33→            if key in seen:
    34→                continue
    35→            ordered.append(key)
    36→            seen.add(key)
    37→    return ",".join(ordered)
    38→
    39→
    40→def render_subjective_scores(entries: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    41→    return ", ".join(
    42→        f"{entry.get('name', 'Subjective')} {float(entry.get('strict', entry.get('score', 100.0))):.1f}%"
    43→        for entry in entries[:max_items]
    44→    )
    45→
    46→
    47→def render_subjective_names(entries: list[dict], *, max_names: int = 3) -> str:
    48→    count = len(entries)
    49→    names = ", ".join(
    50→        str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")) for entry in entries[:max_names]
    51→    )
    52→    if count > max_names:
    53→        names = f"{names}, +{count - max_names} more"
    54→    return names

    57→def coerce_notice_count(value: object) -> int:
    58→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    59→        return 0
    60→    if isinstance(value, int):
    61→        return value
    62→    if isinstance(value, float):
    63→        return int(value)
    64→    if isinstance(value, str):
    65→        try:
    66→            return int(value.strip())
    67→        except ValueError:
    68→            return 0
    69→    return 0
    70→
    71→
    72→def coerce_str_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    73→    if not isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):
    74→        return []
    75→    return [key for key in value if isinstance(key, str) and key]

   105→def _subjective_display_name_from_key(dimension_key: str) -> str:
   106→    return scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(
   107→        dimension_key, dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
   108→    )
   109→
   110→
   111→def subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   112→    dimension_keys: list[str], entries: list[dict]
   113→) -> list[dict]:
   114→    by_key: dict[str, dict] = {}
   115→    for entry in entries:
   116→        for key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   117→            by_key.setdefault(str(key), entry)
   118→
   119→    mapped: list[dict] = []
   120→    for key in dimension_keys:
   121→        if key in by_key:
   122→            mapped.append(by_key[key])
   123→            continue
   124→        mapped.append(
   125→            {
   126→                "name": _subjective_display_name_from_key(key),
   127→                "score": 0.0,
   128→                "strict": 0.0,
   129→                "issues": 0,
   130→                "placeholder": False,
   131→                "cli_keys": [key],
   132→            }
   133→        )
   134→    return mapped
   135→
   136→
   137→def subjective_integrity_followup(
   138→    state: dict,
   139→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   140→    *,
   141→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   142→    max_items: int = 5,
   143→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   144→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   145→    raw_integrity_state = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   146→    integrity_state: dict[str, object] = (
   147→        raw_integrity_state if isinstance(raw_integrity_state, dict) else {}
   148→    )
   149→    status = str(integrity_state.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   150→    raw_target = integrity_state.get("target_score")
   151→    target_display = coerce_target_score(raw_target, fallback=threshold_value)
   152→    matched_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("matched_dimensions", []))
   153→    reset_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("reset_dimensions", []))
   154→
   155→    if status == "penalized" and reset_keys:
   156→        reset_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   157→            reset_keys, subjective_entries
   158→        )
   159→        return {
   160→            "status": "penalized",
   161→            "count": len(reset_keys),
   162→            "target": target_display,
   163→            "entries": reset_entries,
   164→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(reset_entries),
   165→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(reset_entries, max_items=max_items),
   166→        }
   167→
   168→    if status == "warn" and matched_keys:
   169→        matched_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   170→            matched_keys, subjective_entries
   171→        )
   172→        return {
   173→            "status": "warn",
   174→            "count": len(matched_keys),
   175→            "target": target_display,
   176→            "entries": matched_entries,
   177→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(matched_entries),
   178→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(matched_entries, max_items=max_items),
   179→        }
   180→
   181→    at_target = sorted(
   182→        [
   183→            entry
   184→            for entry in subjective_entries
   185→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   186→            and subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score(
   187→                float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   188→                threshold_value,
   189→            )
   190→        ],
   191→        key=lambda entry: str(entry.get("name", "")).lower(),
   192→    )
   193→    if not at_target:
   194→        return None
   195→
   196→    return {
   197→        "status": "at_target",
   198→        "count": len(at_target),
   199→        "target": threshold_value,
   200→        "entries": at_target,
   201→        "rendered": render_subjective_names(at_target),
   202→        "command": subjective_rerun_command(at_target, max_items=max_items),
   203→    }
   204→
   205→
   206→def subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   207→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None,
   208→    *,
   209→    fallback_target: float = 95.0,
   210→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   211→    if not integrity_notice:
   212→        return []
   213→
   214→    status = str(integrity_notice.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   215→    count = coerce_notice_count(integrity_notice.get("count", 0))
   216→    target_display = coerce_target_score(
   217→        integrity_notice.get("target"),
   218→        fallback=fallback_target,
   219→    )
   220→    rendered = str(integrity_notice.get("rendered", "subjective dimensions"))
   221→    command = str(integrity_notice.get("command", ""))
   222→
   223→    if status == "penalized":
   224→        return [
   225→            (
   226→                "red",
   227→                "WARNING: "
   228→                f"{count} subjective dimensions matched target {target_display:.1f} "
   229→                f"and were reset to 0.0 this scan: {rendered}.",
   230→            ),
   231→            (
   232→                "yellow",
   233→                "Anti-gaming safeguard applied. Re-review objectively and import fresh assessments.",
   234→            ),
   235→            ("dim", f"Rerun now: {command}"),
   236→        ]
   237→
   238→    if status == "warn":
   239→        dimension_label = "dimension is" if count == 1 else "dimensions are"
   240→        return [
   241→            (
   242→                "yellow",
   243→                "WARNING: "
   244→                f"{count} subjective {dimension_label} parked on target {target_display:.1f}. "
   245→                "Re-run that review with evidence-first scoring before treating this score as final.",
   246→            ),
   247→            ("dim", f"Next step: {command}"),
   248→        ]
   249→
   250→    if status == "at_target":
   251→        return [
   252→            (
   253→                "yellow",
   254→                "WARNING: "
   255→                f"{count} of your subjective scores matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   256→                f"Can you rerun them by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   257→            ),
   258→        ]
   259→
   260→    return []
   261→
   262→
   263→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   264→# Output-oriented helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_output)
   265→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   266→
   267→
   268→def build_subjective_followup(
   269→    state: dict,
   270→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   271→    *,
   272→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   273→    max_quality_items: int = 3,
   274→    max_integrity_items: int = 5,
   275→) -> SubjectiveFollowup:
   276→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   277→    threshold_label = f"{threshold_value:.1f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
   278→    low_assessed = sorted(
   279→        [
   280→            entry
   281→            for entry in subjective_entries
   282→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   283→            and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < threshold_value
   284→        ],
   285→        key=lambda entry: float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   286→    )
   287→    rendered = render_subjective_scores(low_assessed, max_items=max_quality_items)
   288→    command = subjective_rerun_command(
   289→        low_assessed,
   290→        max_items=max_quality_items,
   291→    )
   292→    integrity_notice = subjective_integrity_followup(
   293→        state,
   294→        subjective_entries,
   295→        threshold=threshold_value,
   296→        max_items=max_integrity_items,
   297→    )
   298→    integrity_lines = subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   299→        integrity_notice,
   300→        fallback_target=threshold_value,
   301→    )
   302→    return SubjectiveFollowup(
   303→        threshold=threshold_value,
   304→        threshold_label=threshold_label,
   305→        low_assessed=low_assessed,
   306→        rendered=rendered,
   307→        command=command,
   308→        integrity_notice=integrity_notice,
   309→        integrity_lines=integrity_lines,
   310→    )
   311→
   312→
   313→def show_subjective_paths(
   314→    state: dict,
   315→    dim_scores: dict,
   316→    *,
   317→    colorize_fn,
   318→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
   319→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   320→) -> None:
   321→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   322→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries_fn(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   323→    if not subjective_entries:
   324→        return
   325→
   326→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   327→        state,
   328→        subjective_entries,
   329→        threshold=threshold_value,
   330→        max_quality_items=3,
   331→        max_integrity_items=5,
   332→    )
   333→    unassessed = sorted(
   334→        [entry for entry in subjective_entries if entry["placeholder"]],
   335→        key=lambda item: item["name"].lower(),
   336→    )
   337→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   338→
   339→    all_findings = state.get("findings", {})
   340→    if not isinstance(all_findings, dict):
   341→        all_findings = {}
   342→    coverage_global, _reason_counts, _holistic_reasons = (
   343→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(all_findings)
   344→    )
   345→
   346→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   347→    if (
   348→        not unassessed
   349→        and not low_assessed
   350→        and stale_count == 0
   351→        and coverage_global <= 0
   352→        and not followup.integrity_notice
   353→    ):
   354→        return
   355→
   356→    # Integrity lines are always preserved (anti-gaming safeguard).
   357→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   358→        print(colorize_fn(f"    {message}", style))
   359→
   360→    # Collapsed summary replacing verbose Subjective path section.
   361→    parts: list[str] = []
   362→    if low_assessed:
   363→        parts.append(f"{len(low_assessed)} below target ({followup.threshold_label}%)")
   364→    if unassessed:
   365→        parts.append(f"{len(unassessed)} unassessed")
   366→    if stale_count:
   367→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   368→    if coverage_global > 0:
   369→        parts.append(f"{coverage_global} files need review")
   370→
   371→    if parts:
   372→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   373→        print(colorize_fn("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   374→
   375→    print()
   376→
   377→
   378→__all__ = [
   379→    "SubjectiveFollowup",
   380→    "build_subjective_followup",
   381→    "coerce_notice_count",
   382→    "coerce_str_keys",
```

```python
def write_packet_snapshot(
    packet: dict,
    *,
    stamp: str,
    review_packet_dir: Path,
    blind_path: Path,
    safe_write_text_fn,
) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """Persist immutable and blind packet snapshots for runner workflows."""
    review_packet_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    packet_path = review_packet_dir / f"holistic_packet_{stamp}.json"
    safe_write_text_fn(packet_path, json.dumps(packet, indent=2) + "\n")
    blind_packet = _build_blind_packet(packet)
    safe_write_text_fn(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
    return packet_path, blind_path
```

- **Lines 675-688** - `_build_blind_packet()` function - **CRITICAL**: Removes score hints but PRESERVES `holistic_context`:
```python
def _build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
    """Return a blind-review packet with score anchoring metadata removed."""
    blind = deepcopy(packet)
    for key in _BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS:
        blind.pop(key, None)

    config = blind.get("config")
    if isinstance(config, dict):
        sanitized = _sanitize_blind_config(config)
        if sanitized:
            blind["config"] = sanitized
        else:
            blind.pop("config", None)
    return blind
```

- **Lines 19-26** - Keys DROPPED from blind packet:
```python
_BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS = {
    "narrative",
    "next_command",
    "score_snapshot",
    "strict_target",
    "strict_target_progress",
    "subjective_at_target",
}
```

**KEY FINDING**: `holistic_context` is **NOT** in `_BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS`, so it **SURVIVES** the blind packet construction.

```python
def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
        effort = "low"
    return [
        "codex",
        "exec",
        "--ephemeral",
        "-C",
        str(repo_root),
        "-s",
        "workspace-write",
        "-c",
        'approval_policy="never"',
        "-c",
        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
        "-o",
        str(output_file),
        prompt,
    ]
```

**KEY FINDING**: The `codex exec` command is invoked with:
- `-C` (working directory): `repo_root` (project root)
- `-s workspace-write`: Grants **FILE READ/WRITE access** to the workspace
- The `prompt` parameter is passed as the final argument (the full prompt text)

The prompt EXPLICITLY includes the file path to the blind packet (`.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json`), and since the agent has workspace-write scope, **IT CAN READ AND PARSE THE BLIND PACKET FILE DIRECTLY**.

## Complete Lifecycle of Subjective Findings When Mechanical Findings Change

### 1. **Subjective Finding Storage in State**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema.py` (lines 125-135)

Subjective assessments are stored with these staleness-tracking fields:

```python
class SubjectiveAssessment(TypedDict, total=False):
    """A single subjective dimension assessment payload."""

    score: float
    integrity_penalty: str | None
    provisional_override: bool
    provisional_until_scan: int
    needs_review_refresh: bool           # Line 132 - Staleness flag
    refresh_reason: str | None           # Line 133 - Why it's stale
    stale_since: str | None              # Line 134 - Timestamp when marked stale
```

These fields are stored in `state["subjective_assessments"][dimension_key]`.

### 2. **How Staleness Fields Are Set**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/resolution.py` (lines 40-81)

The `_mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve()` function marks assessments as stale when review findings are resolved:

```python
def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    status: str,
    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    now: str,
) -> None:
    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.

    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    for a re-review.
    """
    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
        return

    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    for finding in resolved_findings:
        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
            continue
        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
        if dimension:
            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)

    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
        if dimension not in assessments:
            continue

        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
        if isinstance(payload, dict):
            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True         # Line 71
            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"  # Line 72
            payload["stale_since"] = now                   # Line 73
        else:
            assessments[dimension] = {
                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
                "needs_review_refresh": True,              # Line 77
                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",  # Line 78
                "stale_since": now,                        # Line 79
            }
```

This is called from `resolve_findings()` at line 161-166, which gets called both:
- When manually resolving findings via the CLI (`desloppify resolve`)
- When resolving review findings

---

### 3. **Mechanical Finding Changes Do NOT Trigger Auto-Staleness**

**Critical Finding:** There is **NO automatic mechanism** that marks subjective assessments as stale when mechanical findings change (new findings appear, old ones resolve). The staleness marking is **exclusively triggered by review finding status changes**.

Evidence:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge.py` (lines 51-153) — `merge_scan()` handles new/reopened/auto-resolved findings but **does not** call any staleness marking function
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge_findings.py` — `upsert_findings()` and `auto_resolve_disappeared()` update mechanical findings but never touch `subjective_assessments`
- Only `_mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve()` writes the staleness fields, and it only processes `detector == "review"` findings

---

### 4. **When Subjective Assessments Get Marked Stale: The Actual Triggers**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/resolution.py` (line 161-166)

Staleness is set when a **review finding** (not mechanical) is resolved:

```python
_mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    state,
    status=status,  # "fixed", "wontfix", "false_positive", "open"
    resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
    now=now,
)
```

The `refresh_reason` field captures the resolution status:
- `"review_finding_fixed"` — when a review issue is resolved as fixed
- `"review_finding_wontfix"` — when marked won't fix
- `"review_finding_false_positive"` — when marked as false positive

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/state/test_state_internal_direct.py` (lines 158-285)

Tests confirm this behavior:

```python
def test_resolve_fixed_review_marks_assessment_stale_preserves_score():
    """Resolving a review finding as fixed marks assessment stale but keeps score."""
    # ...
    naming = state["subjective_assessments"]["naming_quality"]
    assert naming["score"] == 82          # Score preserved
    assert naming["needs_review_refresh"] is True
    assert naming["refresh_reason"] == "review_finding_fixed"
    assert naming["stale_since"] is not None

def test_resolve_non_review_finding_does_not_mark_stale():
    """Resolving a non-review finding does not touch subjective assessments."""
    # Resolving mechanical findings (e.g., "unused") does NOT mark assessments stale
    naming = state["subjective_assessments"]["naming_quality"]
    assert naming["score"] == 82
    assert "needs_review_refresh" not in naming  # No staleness marker added
```

### 6. **Detection of Staleness: The Stale Flag Computation**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py` (lines 103-107)

The `scorecard_subjective_entries()` function computes the `stale` field by checking for the `needs_review_refresh` flag:

```python
def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    state: dict,
    *,
    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
) -> list[dict]:
    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    
    # ... (line 102-107)
    stale = bool(
        dim_key
        and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
        and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")  # Line 106
    )
    entries.append(
        {
            # ...
            "stale": stale,  # Line 117 - Added to entry
            "dimension_key": dim_key,
            # ...
        }
    )
    return entries
```

This entry with `"stale": True` is then used by all downstream commands (status, show, next, scan).

---

### 7. **No Connection Between Mechanical Finding Changes and Subjective Dimension Staleness**

**Critical Gap:** There is **no automatic system** that connects mechanical finding changes to subjective assessment staleness. For example:

- If new `mutable_globals` findings appear, the `initialization_coupling` subjective dimension is **NOT** automatically marked stale
- If `naming` findings resolve, the `naming_quality` subjective dimension is **NOT** automatically marked stale
- Only explicit review finding resolutions trigger staleness

This means:
- **Mechanical finding changes are never reflected in subjective staleness flags**
- Subjective assessments retain their scores even when the mechanical landscape changes dramatically
- Users must manually re-review if they want updated subjective scores after mechanical findings change
- The only automatic staleness trigger is when you resolve review findings via `desloppify resolve`

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked to implement a detailed plan to "Wire Mechanical Findings into Subjective Review Pipeline" in the desloppify codebase. The goal: every mechanical detector's findings should be aggregated and surfaced as investigative leads for the LLM reviewer, enabling it to identify root causes rather than just symptoms. The plan specified 7 sections: new aggregation module, holistic context enrichment, batch changes, prompt template updates, budget deduplication (deferred), file list, and verification.

   After initial implementation, the user asked two follow-up questions:
   - "And do these fit nicely into the various subjective review topics? And when more things are flagged for a subjective reviewer, do we have a way to flag it?" — Leading to adding `initialization_coupling` to Python/TS dimension defaults and adding a scan evidence summary note to prompts.
   - "And do we definitely include the evidence we have so far in the prompts that we send to the subjective reviewers? And when we rescan and find new things, does it flag that with the subjective findings in the scan results?" — Leading to discovery and fix of the staleness gap where mechanical finding changes never marked subjective assessments as stale.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Mechanical detectors**: 19+ detectors (structural, smells, coupling, naming, etc.) that produce per-file `Finding` TypedDicts in state
   - **Subjective review**: LLM-based holistic review that scores dimensions like `design_coherence`, `initialization_coupling`, `error_consistency`
   - **HolisticContext**: Dataclass with SectionPayload fields (architecture, coupling, conventions, errors, etc.) — the typed contract for holistic review context
   - **Blind packet**: JSON file written to `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` containing full holistic_context minus score-anchoring metadata; subagent reads it from filesystem via `codex exec -s workspace-write`
   - **Investigation batches**: Parallelizable review batches, each targeting specific dimensions with seed files
   - **Concerns**: Ephemeral bridge from mechanical signals to subjective review — `Concern` dataclass with type/file/evidence/fingerprint
   - **Staleness tracking**: `SubjectiveAssessment` TypedDict has `needs_review_refresh`, `refresh_reason`, `stale_since` fields; displayed as `[stale]` in status/show/scan output
   - **Detector→subjective dimension mapping**: New mapping in `merge.py` connecting mechanical detector names to subjective dimension names they provide evidence for
   - **`filter_batches_to_dimensions()`**: Filters batches to only include dimensions in the active set; if a batch's dimensions are all inactive, it's removed entirely

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`intelligence/review/context_holistic/mechanical.py`** (NEW)
     - Core aggregation module. Single-pass bucketing of all open findings, producing 14 evidence clusters
     - Key function: `gather_mechanical_evidence(state: dict) -> dict[str, Any]`
     - Clusters: complexity_hotspots, error_hotspots, mutable_globals, boundary_violations, dead_code, private_crossings, deferred_import_density, duplicate_clusters, naming_drift, flat_dir_findings, large_file_distribution, security_hotspots, signal_density, systemic_patterns
     - Each cluster builder has its own helper function with appropriate thresholds (e.g., error_hotspots requires 3+ exception findings per file, signal_density requires 2+ distinct detectors)

   - **`intelligence/review/_context/models.py`** (MODIFIED)
     - Added `scan_evidence` SectionPayload field to HolisticContext and added to `_SECTION_NAMES` tuple
     ```python
     scan_evidence: SectionPayload = field(
         default_factory=lambda: _empty_section("scan_evidence")
     )
     ```

   - **`intelligence/review/context_holistic/orchestrator.py`** (MODIFIED)
     - Added import of `gather_mechanical_evidence` from `.mechanical`
     - Added call after base context build and before `normalize_sections()`
     - Added `_enrich_sections_from_evidence()` helper that merges evidence into 8 existing sections:
     ```python
     evidence = gather_mechanical_evidence(state)
     if evidence:
         context.scan_evidence = evidence
         _enrich_sections_from_evidence(context, evidence)
     ```
     ```python
     def _enrich_sections_from_evidence(context: HolisticContext, evidence: dict) -> None:
         if "complexity_hotspots" in evidence:
             context.abstractions["complexity_hotspots"] = evidence["complexity_hotspots"]
         if "error_hotspots" in evidence:
             context.errors["exception_hotspots"] = evidence["error_hotspots"]
         if "mutable_globals" in evidence:
             context.errors["mutable_globals"] = evidence["mutable_globals"]
         if "boundary_violations" in evidence:
             context.coupling["boundary_violations"] = evidence["boundary_violations"]
         if "deferred_import_density" in evidence:
             context.dependencies["deferred_import_density"] = evidence["deferred_import_density"]
         if "duplicate_clusters" in evidence:
             context.conventions["duplicate_clusters"] = evidence["duplicate_clusters"]
         if "naming_drift" in evidence:
             context.conventions["naming_drift"] = evidence["naming_drift"]
         if "flat_dir_findings" in evidence:
             context.structure["flat_dir_findings"] = evidence["flat_dir_findings"]
     ```

   - **`intelligence/review/prepare_batches.py`** (MODIFIED)
     - New `_batch_state_design()` function — batch 8: "State & Design Integrity" targeting `["initialization_coupling", "design_coherence"]`
     - Enriched batch 1 (Architecture & Coupling): added `boundary_violations`, `deferred_import_density`
     - Enriched batch 2 (Conventions & Errors): added `exception_hotspots`, `duplicate_clusters`, `naming_drift` directory representatives
     - Enriched batch 3 (Abstractions & Dependencies): added `complexity_hotspots`
     - Enriched batch 7 (Package Organization): added `flat_dir_findings` directory representatives
     - Wired `_batch_state_design` into `build_investigation_batches()` between batch 7 and governance
     - Expanded `batch_concerns` dimensions from `["design_coherence"]` to `["design_coherence", "initialization_coupling"]`

   - **`app/commands/review/batch_prompt_template.py`** (MODIFIED)
     - Added `_render_scan_evidence_focus()` function with dimension-specific guidance for: initialization_coupling, design_coherence, error_consistency, cross_module_architecture, convention_outlier
     - Added `scan_evidence_note` — general advisory telling reviewer about `holistic_context.scan_evidence`
     - Both wired into the prompt output string
     ```python
     scan_evidence_note = (
         "Mechanical scan evidence: The blind packet contains `holistic_context.scan_evidence` "
         "with aggregated signals from all mechanical detectors — including complexity hotspots, "

         "violations, and systemic patterns. Consult this section for investigative leads beyond "
         "the seed files.\n\n"
     )
     ```

   - **`engine/concerns.py`** (MODIFIED)

     ```python

     if len(judgment_dets) < 2 and not elevated:

             continue
     ```

```
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(

   155→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:

   163→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   164→    dim_scores: dict,
   165→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   166→) -> str | None:
   167→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   168→    lowest_name = None
   169→    lowest_score = 101.0
   170→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   171→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   172→        if not ds:
   173→            continue
   174→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   175→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   176→            lowest_score = strict_val
   177→            lowest_name = dim.name
   178→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   179→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   180→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   181→            lowest_score = strict_val
   182→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   183→    return lowest_name
   184→

   186→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   187→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   188→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   189→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   190→        return {}
   191→
   192→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   193→    for finding in findings.values():
   194→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   195→            continue
   196→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   197→            continue
   198→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   199→        dimension = ""
   200→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   201→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   202→        if not dimension:
   203→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   204→        if not dimension:
   205→            continue
   206→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   207→    return counts
   208→

   210→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   211→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   212→) -> None:

   225→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   226→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)

   229→        )
```
