   249→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   250→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   251→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   252→        tier = 4
   253→
   254→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   255→
   256→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   257→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   258→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   259→        placeholder_tag = (
   260→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   261→        )
   262→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   263→        cli_keys = [
   264→            str(key).strip()
   265→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   266→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   267→        ]
   268→        if dim_key:
   269→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   270→        elif cli_keys:
   271→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   272→        else:
   273→            issue_count = 0
   274→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   275→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   276→        print(
   277→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   278→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   279→        )
   280→
   281→
   282→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict]) -> None:
   283→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   284→    print(
   285→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   286→    )
   287→    print(
   288→        colorize(
   289→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   290→            "dim",
   291→        )
   292→    )
   293→    stale_keys = [
   294→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   295→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   296→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   297→    ]
   298→    if stale_keys:
   299→        n = len(stale_keys)
   300→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   301→        print(
   302→            colorize(
   303→                f"  {n} stale subjective dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   304→                f" — run `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}` to re-review",
   305→                "yellow",
   306→            )
   307→        )
   308→
   309→
   310→def show_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   311→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   312→    print()
   313→    bar_len = 20
   314→    print(
   315→        colorize(
   316→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",
   317→            "dim",
   318→        )
   319→    )
   320→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 86, "dim"))
   321→
   322→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   323→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   324→    review_issue_counts = _open_review_issue_counts(state)
   325→
   326→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   327→    _render_subjective_dimensions(
   328→        scorecard_subjective,
   329→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   330→        bar_len=bar_len,
   331→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   332→    )
   333→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective)
   334→    print()
   335→
   336→
   337→def show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   338→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   339→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   340→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   341→    if not lowest_name:
   342→        return
   343→
   344→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   345→    lowest_kind = None
   346→    lowest_score = 101.0
   347→    lowest_issues = 0
   348→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   349→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   350→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   351→            if ds:
   352→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   353→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   354→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   355→            break
   356→    else:
   357→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   358→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   359→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   360→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   361→                lowest_issues = 0
   362→                break
   363→
   364→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   365→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   366→            print(
   367→                colorize(
   368→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   369→                    "cyan",
   370→                )
   371→            )
   372→            print()
   373→            return
   374→
   375→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   376→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   377→        if target_dim:
   378→            impact = 0.0
   379→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   380→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   381→                    {
   382→                        k: {
   383→                            "score": v["score"],
   384→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   385→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   386→                        }
   387→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   388→                        if "score" in v
   389→                    },
   390→                    potentials,
   391→                    det,
   392→                    lowest_issues,
   393→                )
   394→                if impact > 0:
   395→                    break
   396→
   397→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   398→            print(
   399→                colorize(
   400→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   401→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→

   408→def show_subjective_followup(
   409→    state: dict, dim_scores: dict, *, target_strict_score: float
   410→) -> None:
   411→    """Show concrete subjective follow-up commands when applicable."""
   412→    if not dim_scores:
   413→        return
   414→
   415→    subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   416→    if not subjective:
   417→        return
   418→
   419→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   420→        state,
   421→        subjective,
   422→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   423→        max_quality_items=3,
   424→        max_integrity_items=5,
   425→    )
   426→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):

   433→    if not actions:

   447→
   448→
   449→def _collect_structural_areas(
   450→    state: dict,
   451→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   452→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   453→
   454→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   455→    """
   456→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   457→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   458→    )
   459→
   460→    structural = [
   461→        f
   462→        for f in findings.values()
   463→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   464→    ]
   465→
   466→    if len(structural) < 5:
   467→        return None
   468→
   469→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   470→    for f in structural:
   471→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   472→        areas[area].append(f)
   473→
   474→    if len(areas) < 2:
   475→        return None
   476→
   477→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   478→
   479→
   480→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   481→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   482→    rows = []
   483→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   484→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   485→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   486→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   487→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   488→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   489→        rows.append(
   490→            [
   491→                area,
   492→                str(len(area_findings)),
   493→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   494→                str(open_count),
   495→                str(debt_count),
   496→                str(weight),
   497→            ]
   498→        )
   499→    return rows
   500→
   501→
   502→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   503→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   504→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   505→    if remaining > 0:
   506→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   507→
   508→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   509→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   510→    print(
   511→        colorize(
   512→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   513→        )
   514→    )
   515→    print(
   516→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   517→    )
   518→    print()
   519→
   520→
   521→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   522→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   523→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   524→    if sorted_areas is None:
   525→        return
   526→
   527→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   528→    print(
   529→        colorize(
   530→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   531→            "dim",
   532→        )
   533→    )
   534→    print()
   535→
   536→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   537→    print_table(
   538→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   539→    )
   540→
   541→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   542→
   543→
   544→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   545→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   546→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   547→    review_open = [
   548→        f
   549→        for f in findings.values()
   550→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   551→    ]
   552→    if not review_open:
   553→        return
   554→    uninvestigated = sum(
   555→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   556→    )
   557→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   558→    if uninvestigated:
   559→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   560→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   561→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   562→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   563→        print(
   564→            colorize(
   565→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   566→                "dim",
   567→            )
   568→        )
   569→    print()
   570→
   571→

   186→def utc_now() -> str:
   187→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   188→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   189→
   190→
   191→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   192→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   193→    return {
   194→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   195→        "created": utc_now(),
   196→        "last_scan": None,
   197→        "scan_count": 0,
   198→        "overall_score": 0,
   199→        "objective_score": 0,
   200→        "strict_score": 0,
   201→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   202→        "stats": {},

   207→        "subjective_assessments": {},

   210→
   211→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   212→    try:
   213→        parsed = int(value)
   214→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215→        return default
   216→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   217→
   218→
   219→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   220→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   221→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   222→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   223→
   224→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   225→        state["findings"] = {}
   226→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   227→        state["stats"] = {}
   228→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   229→        state["scan_history"] = []
   230→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   231→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   232→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   233→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   234→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   235→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   236→
   237→    findings = state["findings"]
   238→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   239→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   240→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   241→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   242→            continue
   243→
   244→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
   245→        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   246→        finding.setdefault("file", "")
   247→        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
   248→        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   249→        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
   250→        finding.setdefault("detail", {})
   251→        finding.setdefault("status", "open")
   252→        finding["status"] = canonical_finding_status(
   253→            finding.get("status"),
   254→            default="open",
   255→        )
   256→        finding.setdefault("note", None)
   257→        finding.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   258→        finding.setdefault("last_seen", finding["first_seen"])
   259→        finding.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   260→        finding["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   261→            finding.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   262→        )
   263→        finding.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   264→        finding.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   265→        finding.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   266→
   267→    for finding_id in to_remove:
   268→        findings.pop(finding_id, None)
   269→
   270→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   271→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   272→            continue
   273→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   274→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   275→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   276→
   277→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   278→    return None
   279→
   280→
   281→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   282→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   283→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   284→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   285→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   286→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   287→
   288→    findings = state["findings"]
   289→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   290→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   291→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
   292→        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
   293→            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
   294→        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
   295→            raise ValueError(
   296→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
   297→            )
   298→
   299→        tier = finding.get("tier")
   300→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   301→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   302→
   303→        reopen_count = finding.get("reopen_count")
   304→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   305→            raise ValueError(
   306→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
   307→            )
   308→
   309→
   310→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
   311→    """JSON serializer that handles known types and rejects unknowns."""
   312→    if isinstance(obj, set):
   313→        return sorted(obj)
   314→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
   315→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
   316→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
   317→        return obj.isoformat()
   318→    raise TypeError(
   319→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def get_overall_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   324→    """Canonical overall score (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   325→    return state.get("overall_score")
   326→
   327→
   328→def get_objective_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   329→    """Canonical objective score (lenient, mechanical dimensions only)."""
   330→    return state.get("objective_score")
   331→
   332→
   333→def get_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   334→    """Canonical strict score (strict, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   335→    return state.get("strict_score")
   336→
   337→
   338→def get_verified_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   339→    """Strict score that only credits scan-verified fixes.
   340→
   341→    Returns None if no scan-verified score exists yet (fresh state or
   342→    no scan has run). Does not fall back to the unverified strict_score.
   343→    """
   344→    return state.get("verified_strict_score")
   345→

    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    if new_count > 0 or auto_resolved > 0 or reopened_count > 0:
   175→        changed_detectors = set(current_by_detector.keys())
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   204→    return _build_merge_diff(
   205→        new_count=new_count,
   206→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   207→        reopened_count=reopened_count,
   208→        current_ids=current_ids,
   209→        suspect_detectors=suspect_detectors,
   210→        chronic_reopeners=chronic_reopeners,
   211→        skipped_other_lang=skipped_other_lang,
   212→        skipped_out_of_scope=skipped_out_of_scope,
   213→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   214→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   215→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   216→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   217→    )
   218→

1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
   166→

    35→def show_detector_progress(state: dict):

    46→def _dimension_bar(score: float, *, bar_len: int = 15) -> str:
    47→    """Render a score bar consistent with scan detector bars."""
    48→    return presentation_mod.dimension_bar(score, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
    49→
    50→
    51→def scorecard_dimension_entries(
    52→    state: dict,
    53→    *,
    54→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    55→) -> list[dict]:
    56→    """Return scorecard rows with presentation-friendly metadata."""
    57→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    58→    subjective_by_name = {
    59→        entry["name"]: entry
    60→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(
    61→            state,
    62→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    63→        )
    64→    }
    65→    entries: list[dict] = []
    66→    for name, data in rows:
    67→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    68→        subjective_meta = subjective_by_name.get(name)
    69→        is_subjective = subjective_meta is not None
    70→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    71→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    72→        issues = int(data.get("issues", 0))
    73→        checks = int(data.get("checks", 0))
    74→        placeholder = bool(subjective_meta.get("placeholder")) if subjective_meta else False
    75→        not_scanned = bool(
    76→            not is_subjective and not detectors and checks == 0
    77→        )
    78→        carried_forward = bool(
    79→            not is_subjective and data.get("carried_forward")
    80→        )
    81→        dim_key = str(subjective_meta.get("dimension_key", "")) if subjective_meta else ""
    82→        stale = bool(subjective_meta.get("stale")) if subjective_meta else False
    83→        cli_keys = (
    84→            list(subjective_meta.get("cli_keys", []))
    85→            if subjective_meta
    86→            else scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data)
    87→        )
    88→        entries.append(
    89→            {
    90→                "name": name,
    91→                "score": score,
    92→                "strict": strict,
    93→                "issues": issues,
    94→                "checks": checks,
    95→                "subjective": is_subjective,
    96→                "placeholder": placeholder,
    97→                "stale": stale,
    98→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
    99→                "not_scanned": not_scanned,
   100→                "carried_forward": carried_forward,
   101→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   102→            }
   103→        )
   104→    return entries

   106→
   107→def show_scorecard_subjective_measures(state: dict) -> None:

   113→    print(colorize("  Scorecard dimensions (matches scorecard.png):", "dim"))
   114→    for entry in entries:

   134→            + suffix

1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import render as next_render_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    15→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    16→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    23→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    80→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    81→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    82→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    83→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    84→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    85→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    86→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    87→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    88→
    89→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    90→
    91→    queue = build_work_queue(
    92→        state,
    93→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    94→            tier=tier,
    95→            count=count,
    96→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    97→            scope=scope,
    98→            status=status,
    99→            include_subjective=True,
   100→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   101→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   102→            explain=explain,
   103→        ),
   104→    )
   105→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   106→
   107→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   108→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   109→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   110→        state,
   111→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   115→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative
   116→    )
   117→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   118→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   119→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   120→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   121→        state,
   122→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   123→    )
   124→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   125→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   126→    ]
   127→    write_query(payload)
   128→
   129→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   130→    if output_file:
   131→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   132→            output_file,
   133→            payload,
   134→            len(items),
   135→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   136→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   137→        ):
   138→            return
   139→        raise SystemExit(1)
   140→
   141→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   142→        return
   143→
   144→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   145→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   150→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   151→        state.get("findings", {}),
   152→        state.get("scan_path"),
   153→    )
   154→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   155→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain
   156→    )
   157→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   158→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   159→        state,
   160→        dim_scores,
   161→        findings_scoped,
   162→        strict_score=strict_score,
   163→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   164→    )
   165→    print()
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   169→

    55→    )
    56→    print(

    60→            "dim",
    61→        )
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    68→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    69→        for item in grouped_items:
    70→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    71→            print(
    72→                f"    {_tier_label(tier)} [{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
    73→            )
    74→
    75→
    76→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    77→    cmd = (command or "").strip()

    79→
    80→
    81→def _render_item(
    82→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool
    83→) -> None:
    84→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))

   111→
   112→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")

   118→    if detail.get("category"):

   128→        if snippet:
   129→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   130→            print(snippet)
   131→
   132→    if dim_scores:
   133→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   134→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   135→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   136→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   137→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   138→            print(
   139→                colorize(
   140→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   141→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   142→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   143→                    "dim",
   144→                )
   145→            )
   146→
   147→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   148→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   149→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   150→        similar_count = sum(
   151→            1
   152→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   153→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   154→        )
   155→        if similar_count > 1:
   156→            print(
   157→                colorize(
   158→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   159→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   160→                    "cyan",
   161→                )
   162→            )
   163→    if explain:
   164→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   165→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   166→        base = (
   167→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   168→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   169→        )
   170→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   171→        if policy:
   172→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   173→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   174→
   175→
   176→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   177→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   178→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   179→        return
   180→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   181→    if not explain:
   182→        return
   183→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   184→    if available:
   185→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   186→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   187→
   188→
   189→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   190→    if queue.get("items"):
   191→        return False
   192→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   193→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   194→    if tier is not None:
   195→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   196→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   197→        if available:
   198→            commands = " | ".join(
   199→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   200→            )
   201→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   202→    return True
   203→
   204→
   205→def render_terminal_items(
   206→    items: list[dict],
   207→    dim_scores: dict,
   208→    findings_scoped: dict,
   209→    *,
   210→    group: str,
   211→    explain: bool,
   212→) -> None:
   213→    if group != "item":
   214→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   215→        return
   216→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   217→        if idx > 0:
   218→            print()
   219→        label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]" if len(items) > 1 else "  Next item"
   220→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   221→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain)
   222→
   223→
   224→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   225→    if len(items) != 1 or items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   226→        return
   227→    item = items[0]
   228→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   229→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   230→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   231→        primary = item.get(
   232→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   233→        )
   234→        print(f"    {primary}")
   235→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   236→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   240→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   241→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   242→        print(f"    {primary}")
   243→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   244→    else:
   245→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   246→
   247→    print(
   248→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   249→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   250→    )
   251→    print(
   252→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   253→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   254→    )
   255→
   256→
   257→def render_followup_nudges(
   258→    state: dict,
   259→    dim_scores: dict,
   260→    findings_scoped: dict,
   261→    *,
   262→    strict_score: float | None,
   263→    target_strict_score: float,
   264→) -> None:
   265→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   266→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   267→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   268→        state,
   269→        subjective_entries,
   270→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   271→        max_quality_items=3,
   272→        max_integrity_items=5,
   273→    )
   274→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   275→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   276→    if strict_score is not None:
   277→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   278→        if gap > 0:
   279→            print(
   280→                colorize(
   281→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   282→                    "cyan",
   283→                )
   284→            )
   285→        else:
   286→            print(
   287→                colorize(
   288→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   289→                    "green",
   290→                )
   291→            )
   292→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   293→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   294→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   295→
   296→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   297→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   298→        findings_scoped
   299→    )
   300→    parts: list[str] = []
   301→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   302→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   303→    open_review = [
   304→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   305→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   306→    ]
   307→    if low_dims:
   308→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   309→    if unassessed_count:
   310→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   311→    if len(open_review):
   312→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   313→    if coverage_open > 0:
   314→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   315→
   316→    if parts:
   317→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   318→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   319→
   320→
   321→__all__ = [
   322→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   323→    "render_followup_nudges",
   324→    "render_queue_header",
   325→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",

   327→    "scorecard_subjective",
   328→    "show_empty_queue",
   329→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   330→]

1→"""Rendering and scoring helpers for finding work orders."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from datetime import datetime
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.scoring import CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS, HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→
    14→def finding_weight(finding: dict) -> tuple[float, float, str]:
    15→    """Compute (weight, impact_pts, finding_id) for a finding."""
    16→    confidence = finding.get("confidence", "low")
    17→    is_holistic = finding.get("detail", {}).get("holistic", False)
    18→
    19→    weight = CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS.get(confidence, 0.3)
    20→    if is_holistic:
    21→        weight *= HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
    22→
    23→    return weight, weight, finding.get("id", "")
    24→
    25→
    26→def _append_assessment_context(
    27→    lines: list[str], finding: dict, subjective_assessments: dict | None
    28→) -> None:
    29→    if not subjective_assessments:
    30→        return
    31→
    32→    detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    33→    dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    34→    assessment = subjective_assessments.get(dimension)
    35→    if not assessment:
    36→        return
    37→
    38→    display_dimension = dimension.replace("_", " ")
    39→    source = assessment.get("source", "review")
    40→    assessed_at = assessment.get("assessed_at", "")[:10]
    41→    lines.append(
    42→        f"**Dimension assessment**: {display_dimension} — {assessment['score']}/100 "
    43→        f"({source} review, {assessed_at})"
    44→    )
    45→    lines.append(
    46→        "Fixing this issue and re-reviewing should improve the "
    47→        f"{display_dimension} score.\n"
    48→    )
    49→
    50→
    51→def _append_evidence(lines: list[str], detail: dict) -> None:
    52→    evidence = detail.get("evidence", [])
    53→    if evidence:
    54→        lines.append("## Evidence\n")
    55→        for item in evidence:
    56→            lines.append(f"- {item}")
    57→        lines.append("")
    58→
    59→    evidence_lines = detail.get("evidence_lines", [])
    60→    if evidence_lines:
    61→        lines.append("## Code References\n")
    62→        for item in evidence_lines:
    63→            lines.append(f"- {item}")
    64→        lines.append("")
    65→
    66→
    67→def _append_files(
    68→    lines: list[str], finding: dict, detail: dict, is_holistic: bool
    69→) -> None:
    70→    if is_holistic:
    71→        related_files = detail.get("related_files", [])
    72→        if not related_files:
    73→            return
    74→        lines.append("## Files\n")
    75→        for related in related_files:
    76→            lines.append(f"- `{related}`")
    77→        lines.append("")
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    file_path = finding.get("file", "")
    81→    if file_path and file_path != ".":
    82→        lines.append("## Files\n")
    83→        lines.append(f"- `{file_path}`")
    84→        lines.append("")
    85→
    86→
    87→def _append_investigation(lines: list[str], finding_id: str, detail: dict) -> bool:
    88→    investigation = detail.get("investigation")
    89→    if not investigation:
    90→        return False
    91→
    92→    investigated_at = detail.get("investigated_at", "")
    93→    date_suffix = ""
    94→    if investigated_at:
    95→        try:
    96→            date_suffix = (
    97→                f" ({datetime.fromisoformat(investigated_at).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')})"
    98→            )
    99→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   100→            logger.debug(
   101→                "Invalid investigated_at value %r on %s: %s",
   102→                investigated_at,
   103→                finding_id,
   104→                exc,
   105→            )
   106→            date_suffix = " (invalid date)"
   107→
   108→    lines.append(f"## Investigation{date_suffix}\n")
   109→    lines.append(f"{investigation}\n")
   110→    return True
   111→
   112→
   113→def _append_footer(
   114→    lines: list[str],
   115→    *,
   116→    has_investigation: bool,
   117→    lang_name: str,
   118→    finding_id: str,
   119→    number: int | None,
   120→) -> None:
   121→    if has_investigation:
   122→        lines.append("## Ready to Fix\n")
   123→        lines.append("When done:\n")
   124→        lines.append("```bash")
   125→        lines.append(f'desloppify resolve fixed "{finding_id}"')
   126→        lines.append("```\n")
   127→        return
   128→
   129→    lines.append("## Status: Needs Investigation\n")
   130→    lines.append("Investigate the files above, then resolve with a note:\n")
   131→    lines.append("```bash")
   132→    lines.append(
   133→        f'desloppify resolve fixed "{finding_id}" --note "description of fix"'
   134→    )
   135→    lines.append("```\n")
   136→    lines.append("Or save detailed analysis first:\n")
   137→    lines.append("```bash")
   138→    lines.append(
   139→        f'desloppify show "{finding_id}" --notes analysis.md'
   140→    )
   141→    lines.append("```\n")
   142→
   143→
   144→def render_issue_detail(
   145→    finding: dict,
   146→    lang_name: str,
   147→    number: int | None = None,
   148→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   149→) -> str:
   150→    """Render one finding as a markdown work order from state."""
   151→    finding_id = finding["id"]
   152→    detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   153→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   154→    is_holistic = detail.get("holistic", False)
   155→    is_review = detector in ("review", "concerns")
   156→
   157→    # Derive dimension: from detail for review, from registry for mechanical.
   158→    raw_dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   159→    if raw_dimension:
   160→        dimension = raw_dimension.replace("_", " ")
   161→    else:
   162→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   163→        dimension = meta.dimension.replace("_", " ") if meta else "unknown"
   164→
   165→    confidence = finding.get("confidence", "low")
   166→
   167→    parts = finding_id.split("::")
   168→    identifier = parts[-2] if len(parts) >= 3 else finding.get("file", "unknown")
   169→    weight, impact_pts, _ = finding_weight(finding)
   170→    label = "+++" if weight >= 8 else "++" if weight >= 5 else "+"
   171→
   172→    lines: list[str] = []
   173→    lines.append(f"# {dimension}: {identifier}\n")
   174→    lines.append(f"**Finding**: `{finding_id}`  ")
   175→    if not is_review:
   176→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   177→        detector_display = meta.display if meta else detector
   178→        lines.append(f"**Detector**: {detector_display} | **Confidence**: {confidence}  ")
   179→    else:
   180→        lines.append(f"**Dimension**: {dimension} | **Confidence**: {confidence}  ")
   181→    lines.append(f"**Score impact**: {label} (~{impact_pts:.1f} pts)\n")
   182→
   183→    _append_assessment_context(lines, finding, subjective_assessments)
   184→
   185→    lines.append("## Problem\n")
   186→    lines.append(f"{finding.get('summary', '')}\n")
   187→
   188→    _append_evidence(lines, detail)
   189→
   190→    suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   191→    if suggestion:
   192→        lines.append("## Suggested Fix\n")
   193→        lines.append(f"{suggestion}\n")
   194→
   195→    _append_files(lines, finding, detail, is_holistic)
   196→
   197→    reasoning = detail.get("reasoning", "")
   198→    if reasoning:
   199→        lines.append("## Why This Matters\n")
   200→        lines.append(f"{reasoning}\n")
   201→
   202→    has_investigation = _append_investigation(lines, finding_id, detail)
   203→    _append_footer(
   204→        lines,
   205→        has_investigation=has_investigation,
   206→        lang_name=lang_name,
   207→        finding_id=finding_id,
   208→        number=number,
   209→    )
   210→    return "\n".join(lines)
   211→
```

"""Verify that mechanical finding changes mark subjective assessments stale."""

    def test_new_findings_mark_assessments_stale(self):
        from desloppify.engine._state.merge import merge_scan
        from desloppify.engine._state.schema import empty_state

        state = empty_state()
        state["subjective_assessments"] = {
            "design_coherence": {"score": 75.0},
            "initialization_coupling": {"score": 80.0},
        }
        new_findings = [
            _finding(id="s1", detector="structural", file="big.py",
                     detail={"signals": {"loc": 500}}),
        ]
        merge_scan(state, new_findings)

        dc = state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]
        assert dc["needs_review_refresh"] is True
        assert dc["refresh_reason"] == "mechanical_findings_changed"
        assert dc["stale_since"] is not None

    def test_unrelated_detector_doesnt_mark_stale(self):
        from desloppify.engine._state.merge import merge_scan
        from desloppify.engine._state.schema import empty_state

        state = empty_state()
        state["subjective_assessments"] = {
            "initialization_coupling": {"score": 80.0},
        }
        # 'unused' has no subjective dimension mapping
        new_findings = [
            _finding(id="u1", detector="unused", file="a.py"),
        ]
        merge_scan(state, new_findings)

        ic = state["subjective_assessments"]["initialization_coupling"]
        assert "needs_review_refresh" not in ic

    def test_no_change_doesnt_mark_stale(self):
        from desloppify.engine._state.merge import merge_scan
        from desloppify.engine._state.schema import empty_state

        state = empty_state()
        state["subjective_assessments"] = {
            "design_coherence": {"score": 75.0},
        }
        # Empty scan — no new, no resolved
        merge_scan(state, [])

        dc = state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]
        assert "needs_review_refresh" not in dc

    def test_already_stale_not_overwritten(self):
        from desloppify.engine._state.merge import merge_scan
        from desloppify.engine._state.schema import empty_state

        state = empty_state()
        state["subjective_assessments"] = {
            "design_coherence": {
                "score": 75.0,
                "needs_review_refresh": True,
                "refresh_reason": "review_finding_fixed",
                "stale_since": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
            },
        }
        new_findings = [
            _finding(id="s1", detector="structural", file="big.py",
                     detail={"signals": {"loc": 500}}),
        ]
        merge_scan(state, new_findings)

        dc = state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]
        # Should keep original reason, not overwrite
        assert dc["refresh_reason"] == "review_finding_fixed"

    def test_global_mutable_config_marks_init_coupling(self):
        from desloppify.engine._state.merge import merge_scan
        from desloppify.engine._state.schema import empty_state

        state = empty_state()
        state["subjective_assessments"] = {
            "initialization_coupling": {"score": 80.0},
        }
--
        assert ic["needs_review_refresh"] is True
        assert ic["refresh_reason"] == "mechanical_findings_changed"

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    return (
    93→        detector == scope
    94→        or filepath == scope

    99→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   100→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   101→
   102→
   103→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   104→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   105→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   106→    target = cleaned.lower()
   107→
   108→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   109→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   110→            return str(dim_key)
   111→    return slugify(cleaned)
   112→
   113→
   114→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   115→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   116→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   117→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   118→    return {
   119→        cleaned.lower(),
   120→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   121→        slugify(cleaned),
   122→        canonical.lower(),
   123→        slugify(canonical),
   124→    }
   125→
   126→
   127→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   128→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   129→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   130→    if not lang:
   131→        return None
   132→
   133→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   134→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   138→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   142→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   143→        return None
   144→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   145→
   146→
   147→def primary_command_for_finding(
   148→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   149→) -> str:
   150→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   151→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   152→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   153→        available_fixers = [
   154→            fixer
   155→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   156→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   157→        ]
   158→        if available_fixers:
   159→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   160→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   161→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   162→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   163→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   164→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'

   179→        [REDACTED](name)

   184→                continue
   185→            aliases.add(key)
   186→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   187→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   188→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   189→        for alias in aliases:
   190→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   191→    return scores

## Summary: How Subjective Assessment Staleness is Displayed to Users

I've completed a thorough search of the desloppify codebase to understand how subjective assessment staleness is surfaced. Here's what I found:

### 1. **Staleness Metadata Structure** (`engine/_state/schema.py`)

The `SubjectiveAssessment` TypedDict defines three new fields:
- `needs_review_refresh: bool` — Flag indicating staleness
- `refresh_reason: str | None` — Reason for staleness (e.g., "mechanical_findings_changed")
- `stale_since: str | None` — ISO timestamp when marked stale

### 2. **Staleness Detection & Marking** (`engine/_state/merge.py`)

Staleness is marked automatically via `_mark_stale_on_mechanical_change()` which is called during `merge_scan()`. It maps mechanical detectors to affected subjective dimensions:

- Detectors like `structural`, `smells`, `coupling`, `dupes`, `naming`, `flat_dirs`, `orphaned`, `uncalled_functions`, `responsibility_cohesion`, and `cycles` trigger staleness on their mapped dimensions
- Only marks dimensions that already have assessments (doesn't create new entries)
- Does NOT overwrite if already marked stale (preserves original reason)

### 3. **Staleness Display in Status Output** (`app/commands/status_parts/render.py`)

**[stale] marker in dimension table (line 258):**
```python
stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
```
- Yellow "stale" tag appears next to subjective dimension scores in the status dimension table
- Example: `Design Coherence    ├─────┤   75.0%   75.0%  T4  review  ← [stale]`

**Summary footer hint (lines 293-307):**
- Counts stale dimensions and provides a hint command to re-review them
- Example: `1 stale subjective dimension — run 'desloppify review --prepare --dimensions design_coherence' to re-review`

### 4. **Staleness Display in Scan Output** (`app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_dimensions.py`)

**[stale — re-review] suffix (line 129):**
```python
elif entry.get("stale"):
    suffix = colorize("  [stale — re-review]", "yellow")
```
- Shows inline in the scorecard dimensions listing during scan

**Re-review command hint (lines 136-146):**
- After dimension table, provides grouped re-review commands for all stale dimensions
- Example: `1 stale subjective dimension — run 'desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import --dimensions design_coherence' to re-review`

### 5. **Staleness is Projected to Scorecard** (`engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py`)

**In `scorecard_subjective_entries()` (lines 103-107):**
```python
stale = bool(
    dim_key
    and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
    and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
)
```
- Checks `needs_review_refresh` flag and exposes it in the returned entries
- Used by all downstream display code (status, scan, next)

### 6. **Staleness in the `next` Command**

**Coverage in `next_parts/render.py` (lines 257-319):**
- The `render_followup_nudges()` function shows subjective dimension status
- Currently shows: "N dimensions below target", "N unassessed", "N review findings open", "N files need review"
- **Gap: Does NOT explicitly surface "N dimensions are stale" in the nudges section**
- Subjective items are built via `build_subjective_items()` which filters by score threshold but doesn't distinguish stale vs. fresh

### 7. **Staleness in Resolve Command** (`app/commands/resolve/render.py`)

**Post-resolve re-review hint:**
- When a review finding is resolved, the code extracts all stale dimensions from `subjective_assessments`
- Suggests re-running review with: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {stale_dims}`
- Example: "Next subjective step: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions design_coherence,abstraction_fitness`"

### 8. **Work Queue Integration** (`engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`)

**Subjective items in work queue (lines 194-280):**
- `build_subjective_items()` creates queue items for subjective dimensions below a score threshold
- Items include detail fields: `dimension_name`, `dimension`, `issues`, `strict_score`, `open_review_findings`, `cli_keys`
- **Gap: Does NOT include staleness flag in queue items** — stale dimensions are queued based on score alone, not prioritized differently

### 9. **Staleness in Narrative/Actions**

**Not explicitly surfaced in narrative (intelligence/narrative/):**
- Narrative reminders include `review_stale` (for review cache staleness, lines 320-322) but NOT for subjective assessment staleness
- No narrative action is generated to say "prioritize re-reviewing these N stale dimensions"

---

## Key Findings

| Surface | Where Staleness Appears | How It's Shown | Actionability |
|---------|-------------------------|----------------|---------------|
| **Status command** | Dimension health table | Yellow `[stale]` tag + summary footer hint | Yes: provides re-review command |
| **Scan command** | Scorecard dimensions listing | Yellow `[stale — re-review]` suffix + grouped commands | Yes: provides batched re-review command |
| **Next command** | Work queue / followup nudges | Subjective items queued by score, staleness NOT called out | Partial: dimensions are in queue but staleness not highlighted |
| **Show/Resolve** | Post-action suggestions | Re-review hint after resolve | Yes: provides dimension-specific command |
| **Work queue items** | Internal queue data | Staleness not included in queue item detail | No: can't distinguish stale from fresh |
| **Narrative/Actions** | Agent coaching | No explicit mention of stale dimensions | No: not surfaced |

## Gaps Identified

1. **Next command doesn't call out staleness** — `render_followup_nudges()` doesn't mention "N dimensions are stale" even though the data is available
2. **Work queue doesn't prioritize stale** — subjective items are ranked by score threshold alone, not by freshness
3. **Narrative doesn't flag stale assessments** — no action generated to prioritize re-review of stale dimensions
4. **No "stale" vs "unassessed" distinction in next** — both are low-scoring, but stale=old-valid, unassessed=unreviewed
